# Ronnie R-1 experiment ledger

Research goal: `01a11518-fb33-7853-a31c-b8c43df5be60`. The exit targets are a reproducible, fee- and funding-inclusive **single-account** annualized return above 20%, a closed-trade win rate near 60%, and then the highest defensible Sharpe ratio, with drawdown reported. No exchange trading is authorized.

Operational comparison convention: annualize the native final-equity ratio over actual UTC calendar days using 365 days/year; one closed native position is one trade even with partial exits; a win has positive native position `realized_pnl`, which includes fill commissions in settlement currency and funding adjustments applied to that position. Funding also affects the native account equity. For mechanism screening, interpret "near 60%" as 55-65% inclusive, declared before selecting any new strategy variant. Report the native daily-return Sharpe using both 252 and 365 annualization; prefer 365 for this continuous crypto market. This convention does not turn development data into independent evidence.

Product and process findings for later blueprint reconciliation are in `docs/plans/r1-native-rd-findings.zh.md`. The [record ownership and F cross-index](RECORD_INDEX.md) was added after S46 for navigation; it does not change the date or evidence level of earlier registrations.

This file records source observations, diagnostic hypotheses, exact data/result locators, outcomes, and next decisions. The 2025-10-07 to 2026-10-07 Binance futures sample and the 2026-09 to 2026-10 sample have already been inspected. Further results from them are development evidence, even if the code was registered before a particular rerun. All attempted variants and reads remain visible here; overlapping slices are not independent holdouts. Use predeclared mechanism changes, paired native replay, multiple-testing-aware uncertainty and later forward evidence to address this exposure. Lack of a fresh historical holdout does not stop the research.

A rejected diagnostic closes only the tested rule, data claim, or economic claim. For a still-plausible parent mechanism, record a distinct child explanation and its falsifiable source-case or native-event prediction before another result read; retain the parent's failure and every child attempt. Do not rescue a failed rule by moving its threshold after seeing the exposed year. A branch may stop when evidence defeats the mechanism, a declared resource bound is met, or a more informative branch takes priority, with that reason recorded. This is an Agent research decision, not an automatic experiment-expansion service.

## Source and baseline identity

- Frozen prototype reference: Git `0725a7b3f89902e27cd421a18b4b879a13268534`, `research/ronnie/loop/family_r.py` and `research/ronnie/RONNIE_2024_RULES.md`. It is a rule source, not a native fill/PnL authority.

R-1u uses closed daily pivot breaks, a limit at the prior pivot high/low, an ATR-zone stop, a single frozen 2R target, ten-day order validity and a 60-day position limit. Its 37 independent 100,000-USDT accounts gained 6,032.44693685 USDT in aggregate (+0.1630391% against 3.7m), with 1,600 closed trades and 36.75% winners. This is **not** a single-account portfolio return or target-gate observation. Per-contract source/input digests and native order/fill/account reports are under each `result_stop_market` directory.
- The 2026-09-01 to 2026-10-07 1-minute 37-coin baseline is `/tmp/r1-37-2026oct7/summary_stop_fix.json`, with its instrument/quantity manifest at `/tmp/r1-37-2026oct7/manifest.json`. This baseline predates the daily-close boundary repair below.
- The reused one-year five-minute minute catalogs, daily warmup catalogs and fixed-quantity CSV are identified by `results/2026-10-07-input-identity.json` (SHA-256 `ce9963ca68c66d34af74dbdbfff484f320a622fa73afe64844518aed354ec9fc`). It contains a per-coin tree digest using sorted relative paths and file-byte hashes, not just row counts. Subsequent variants must use the same input identity unless a documented data repair creates a new version.

## Source observation S01: R-1u covers one part of Ronnie's method

The author's 2024-10-28 original video was parsed through this repository's `video-note-mcp` from [the primary video file](https://video-ideas.tradingview.com/1/1416280-Bf_rqWB7C_1tcMiE.mp4). The complete timestamped transcript, generated note and screenshots are retained at `/Users/vx/.local/share/bilibili-note-mcp/notes/note-00e13dbc423b4fdea073b515bff98c6e/` (fast profile; no audio review). Relevant evidence:

- 01:01-02:05: BTC is treated as a 4-hour range, trading the range edges, with a more attractive long when a rising trend line meets a horizontal role-reversal support. A daily pivot-cross-only R-1u does not represent the range or confluence choice.
- 02:10-03:15: ETH wick-only penetration does not constitute a break; support/resistance is an area rather than an exact price. The current stop zone has an ATR cap, but the limit is placed at one pivot price and has no 4-hour confirmation.
- 04:10-05:28: on APE/DYDX, stop location is below the large breakout candle or prior structural low, and position size is set from the money risk implied by that stop. The current replay fixes quantity per coin from a prior close, without risk-based sizing.
- 06:05-06:50: DOGE's 4-hour pullback combines Fibonacci 38.2% with a previous high. He names several **alternative** trades depending on the future path, including a breakout retest. These are not one unconditional daily order rule.

These observations are human/source claims, not evidence of profitable execution. The note's generic-source metadata lacks author/date; identity and date above come from the frozen TradingView video-idea record at the source commit. Bilibili and YouTube dedicated acquisition attempts for this research failed (`BV1baduBmEL1`: metadata code -404; `doz72-I2LKM`: same-host Googlevideo HTTP 302 refused by the adapter). Do not infer their spoken rules from titles or AI summaries.

## Source observation S02: structural stop and stepped targets

The author's 2025-02-25 [original video](https://video-ideas.tradingview.com/1/1416280-nNo75ZxC6tXOfNoT.mp4) was parsed through `video-note-mcp`; complete transcript, note and screenshots are at `/Users/vx/.local/share/bilibili-note-mcp/notes/note-f112d7ceacb147d180327781b0db6d58/` (fast profile). At 00:24-00:40 he reviews a 4-hour short from horizontal resistance whose first two targets were reached. At 00:58-01:24 he separately considers a long at a support band, puts the stop below the band, and declines to chase a short. At 02:34-02:59 he names a trend-line plus horizontal role-reversal support for LTC. The original frame at 00:31 (`/Users/vx/.local/share/bilibili-note-mcp/notes/note-f112d7ceacb147d180327781b0db6d58/images/source-1-3.png`) visibly labels the BTC short with a 3% position, stop 99,688 and three targets 94,000 / 92,000 / 90,000. This is direct evidence of a stepped exit plan and context-dependent direction, while actual order execution remains unverified. The screenshot uses a BTC spot chart as a drawing source; Binance futures remains the replay market.

## Source observation S03: trend and range context

The author's 2025-02-21 [original video](https://video-ideas.tradingview.com/0/0-aKWKvFZAvw_fZ4AQ.mp4) was parsed through `video-note-mcp`; transcript, note and 15 source screenshots are at `/Users/vx/.local/share/bilibili-note-mcp/notes/note-3992fe17853e4cd199271658d61e2b5a/` (fast profile). At 00:00-01:28 he says that an OM 4-hour sequence of rising lows and new highs calls for long exposure on a pullback, not a temporary countertrend short. At 03:09-03:49 he warns against buying a single extended up candle and waits for consolidation or support. At 04:03-05:05 he treats BTC as a larger-range market and avoids the middle of the range. At 05:33-05:45 he gives ETH a different bias from its descending trend line. These are discretionary context choices absent from R-1u's daily-pivot trend state. Any mechanical encoding needs source-fidelity checks against chart examples before its economic result is attributed to Ronnie.

## Source observation S04: range context and multi-timeframe targets

The author's 2025-01-03 [original video](https://video-ideas.tradingview.com/1/1416280-CE95nknsvjzFWTbV.mp4) was parsed through this repository's `video-note-mcp`; timestamped transcript and 11 source frames are at `/Users/vx/.local/share/bilibili-note-mcp/notes/note-abe46396788340e491006e35561f6e42/` (fast profile, no audio review). At 00:29-01:10 he describes BTC as a four-hour range and its upper resistance area as both a held long's target and a possible later short zone. At 03:39-05:34 he walks ETH through a break/retest of a smaller range, then successive four-hour, daily and weekly resistance targets; the 04:24 original frame (`/Users/vx/.local/share/bilibili-note-mcp/notes/note-abe46396788340e491006e35561f6e42/images/source-1-1.png`) visibly shows zones and conditional alternative arrows on a four-hour ETH chart. At 06:06-07:12 he says a correct direction can still lose with the wrong entry or position size. At 09:08-09:19 he repeats that a range-middle location calls for no trade, depending on whether one uses range quarters or trend-line analysis. The 09:19 frame (`/Users/vx/.local/share/bilibili-note-mcp/notes/note-abe46396788340e491006e35561f6e42/images/source-1-10.png`) is a gold daily chart, so it is source-method context, not a Binance futures observation. This further supports that daily pivot and fixed 2R alone are a partial mechanical encoding, and that a source-faithful multi-timeframe candidate needs explicit chart-case checks before a PnL claim.

## Diagnostic D01: apparent 1-minute / 5-minute signal mismatch

**Hypothesis registered before repair:** different bar resolution or daily warmup could change the BTC/ETH daily signal state in the same September-October 2026 interval. Pair runs held the instrument, trade start, end, quantity and native engine fixed, changing only the minute interval and its prepared catalog. The exact native result locators are `/tmp/r1-rd-5m-pair/{BTC,ETH}` and `/tmp/r1-37-2026oct7/{BTC,ETH}/result_window_stop_fix`. One-minute and five-minute daily OHLC matched on all 37 common September-October days; five-minute aggregation and Binance external daily OHLC matched on all 329 preceding days compared. Yet BTC had 25 versus 28 signals and ETH 42 versus 49.

An instrumented replay recorded each Strategy daily bar at `/tmp/r1-rd-daily-audit-{1m,5m}.csv`. Its 2,586 BTC daily OHLC, ATR and trend states match exactly; only timestamps differ for warmup daily bars. The external 2026-08-31 daily candle is stamped `2026-08-31 23:59:59.999 UTC`, while the internally aggregated close is stamped `2026-09-01 00:00:00 UTC`. At a `2026-09-01 00:00` trade-start boundary, the old comparison discarded three BTC and seven ETH already-known signals only in the 1-minute run. This is a replay boundary defect, not market-price disagreement.

**Repair and paired read:** `strategy.py` now normalizes external 23:59:59.999 closes to their midnight availability before comparing with trade start or arming a signal. New one-minute results are `/tmp/r1-rd-boundary-fix-{BTC,ETH}-1m`. BTC now has 28 signals, seven positions and 99,943.40262727 USDT final equity; ETH has 49 signals, six positions and 100,050.94670389 USDT final equity. Each equals its paired five-minute native result exactly, including applied funding. This establishes parity for these two samples only; it does not prove all instruments or intrabar paths equivalent.

**37-coin repair read:** all 37 one-minute runs completed with the same prepared catalogs, quantity manifest, trade start and report boundary. Per-contract native reports and a complete manifest are at `/tmp/r1-rd-boundary-fix-37/`. The full September 1-October 7 aggregate native equity change is +2,275.31357956 USDT, versus +2,048.60463262 before repair; closed trades are 262 (98 winners, 37.4046%) versus 255 (94 winners, 36.8627%). Seven coins' final equity changed; the October 6-7 mark-to-market window remains -1,045.06240296 USDT in both runs. Thus the boundary defect changed the longer comparison but does not explain the acute October loss. This is still an aggregate of 37 separate accounts.

## Diagnostic D02: annual return denominator and outcome decomposition

The one-year baseline used 37 independent 100,000-USDT native accounts. Across 1,600 closed positions, mean entry notional was 493.7110 USDT; completed-position time-weighted entry notional averaged 8,968.86 USDT across the 37 sleeves, 0.2424% of the 3.7m denominator. This explains why account return is small, without establishing a higher attainable return at different capital or risk. Native fills charged 464.0862 USDT in commissions; settled funding across the 37 results was -224.4088 USDT. Closed long positions: 864 trades, -1,264.3397 USDT; shorts: 736 trades, +7,167.6396 USDT. A fixed 2026 universe was backcast into 2025, so point-in-time membership is unproven. Position sizing was frozen against a 2026-08-31 or 2025-08-31 price depending on the run, not the current stop distance.

**Next decision:** use a single native portfolio account and explicit risk budget to measure return, drawdown and Sharpe on the same denominator; first validate the 37-coin boundary repair and native multi-instrument replay. Preserve R-1u as the diagnostic baseline. Register source-derived 4-hour confluence/structural-stop and R-1s exit hypotheses separately before scoring them; a selected variant must be compared against cash, buy-and-hold and matched random-entry controls under the same account/cost model. Record every variant and failed run here.

## Diagnostic D03: one-account 37-coin annual baseline

**Predeclared purpose:** replace the 37 independent-account denominator with one native margin account, holding R-1u signals, the existing fixed quantities, one-year sample and five-minute prepared inputs fixed. `run_portfolio.py` streams all 37 cached LAST/MARK/funding series through one `BacktestEngine` account with 100,000 USDT, 1x default leverage, and one native Strategy per coin. BTC/ETH pilot `/tmp/r1-rd-portfolio-pilot-explicit/summary.json` reconciled its absolute +120.68306160 USDT PnL with the two separate native accounts, before the full replay. The complete run is `/tmp/r1-rd-portfolio-37/`; its summary is retained in `results/2026-10-07-37coin-shared-baseline.json` (SHA-256 `830999c9fbf49c3362262d34ed22163318d9599877ad5b4dc16f99b326dd561f`). Native positions and returns files have SHA-256 `fedb71ed713c908eb4c313d21c73d5aca82fe4d235fa214f5f79309054d61fc1` and `f361b6e3f5b43acb978604002eb773c71bb55c5fd27aa29ddc6016a014905bda` respectively.

**Result:** 2025-10-07 00:00 to 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC; 3,893,214 LAST and 3,893,214 MARK bars, 42,779 funding records, 37 instruments. Native final equity 105,972.39044515 USDT; period +5.97239%, annualized +5.96643%; 1,602 closed positions, 588 positive (36.7041%), 16 still open. Native daily-return Sharpe (252) 0.852947 and (365) 1.026522, and native maximum daily-close drawdown -2.69123%; 366 daily return points compound exactly to the final equity ratio. Fees and funding are in the native account result. The annual return and win-rate Goal gates both fail. Unlike 37 separate accounts, this run has one capital denominator; its absolute gain is 60.05649 USDT below the earlier separate-account sum, and the closed-trade count differs by two. We have not attributed that difference to a single mechanism.

**Execution integrity read:** the native order report contains 18 rejected contingent exit orders after parent positions closed, plus two **denied entry orders** (FIL on 2026-06-16 and ETC on 2026-08-31). The log gives `EXPIRE_TIME_IN_PAST` for both. These are not evidence of insufficient margin. The Strategy could release a waiting signal at a daily close before the same-timestamp minute-bar expiry pass and lacked an `on_order_denied` cleanup callback. This is an implementation defect. The result above is a diagnostic baseline, not a clean candidate score; register and repair D04 before comparing variants.

## Diagnostic D04: expired waiting candidates at a daily close

**Hypothesis registered before repair:** filter waiting candidates against the native clock at release, and clear a denied entry through the native order event callback. On the same cached data and same portfolio configuration, the two `EXPIRE_TIME_IN_PAST` denials should disappear. Compare per-coin signals, positions, final account equity and returns series with D03; any differences must be attributed, not silently absorbed. This is an execution-correctness replay, not a strategy parameter search. Candidate, run and result locators are to be added after the paired run.

**Paired repair read:** `strategy.py` prunes expired waiting signals using the native clock when releasing candidates and handles `on_order_denied` to clear the native order slot. The same one-year, 37-coin, 100,000-USDT native replay is `/tmp/r1-rd-portfolio-37-expiry-fix/`; its summary is retained in `results/2026-10-07-37coin-shared-expiry-fix.json` (SHA-256 `ac90e49bf0be6fab1f27c635f5d8b78140bd0298acfe9e6ff791284c02823c83`). The native returns, positions and fills SHA-256 values are `56af34560f386dc130c695795a6fdb4040f67fb4f9d42d66bec38b62f2d3c6b9`, `31b67b2253a4f0515b0ec991be9db621fbddffbd424bb6299ba9db358462c246`, and `6d3272f5d18720eccd7a808c670317ddb009ec5158c0e42d6bcfd41b45727d7c` respectively. Denied orders fell from two to zero; no `EXPIRE_TIME_IN_PAST` appears in the new log. All 11,471 signal counts remained unchanged. FIL gained 16 native positions and ETC gained eight; the old denied entry left those per-coin slots unable to release later candidates. The other 35 coins' position counts did not change. The repair therefore changed 24 positions, 23 of which were closed at the end, rather than just two attempts.

**Clean diagnostic baseline:** final equity 106,004.83006702 USDT; annualized return +5.998838%; 1,625 closed positions, 595 positive (36.6154%), 17 open; native daily-return Sharpe (252) 0.854814 and (365) 1.028769; daily-close native max drawdown -2.69123%. It still fails both economic Goal gates. The report has 19 rejected native orders, which are contingent exits after position closure; no entry denial remains. This D04 result supersedes D03 for subsequent mechanism comparisons while D03 remains in the attempt ledger. Minute-sampled drawdown is still unverified.

## Candidate H01: source-aligned risk sizing, after D04

**Pre-registration before an economic read:** Ronnie's 2024-10-28 source at 04:10-05:28 sizes the position from the stop distance and money risk, while the current baseline fixes each coin's quantity from a prior price. In a separate named variant, keep the same R-1u signal, stop, entry, target, data and native venue/account; at each entry submission choose the smaller of (a) quantity placing 0.25% of current native portfolio equity at the planned stop and (b) 5% of native equity in entry notional per coin, rounded down to the native Instrument size increment. Skip and count quantities below the native minimum. The cap limits gross deployment if a very tight stop would imply a large notional. `0.25%` and `5%` are frozen here before any result, not selected from the one-year score.

This directly tests the denominator/capital-use explanation for the return gate and should not mechanically improve the signal's win rate. Compare against the D04 clean baseline on the same one-account replay, including accepted/rejected/denied orders, capital use, net annual return, position-level win rate, both native daily Sharpe conventions, and daily-close drawdown. An apparent gain is still development evidence on a seen year; the result cannot alone close the Goal, especially if win rate remains outside 55-65%.

**Pilot and full read:** BTC/ETH pilot `/tmp/r1-rd-risk-size-pilot-BTC-ETH/` completed with 115 closed positions, 40 winners and zero denied orders, establishing the dynamic size can execute through native Instrument/Portfolio APIs without changing the signal count. The full 37-coin run is `/tmp/r1-rd-risk-size-37/`; its summary is retained in `results/2026-10-07-37coin-risk-sized-h01.json` (SHA-256 `42dd8d1ce3e36a1e039ab43907f7bba1b9dc32ec260fe01f1de3027a9ce55baa`). Native returns, positions and fills hashes are `7b2a6649953c28ccb5b1ba00fcb24b1fe2edbd866cb9884f01e9ca3be595e9a5`, `04554c8076d84f81136947f46f31950a178d0c2bc2826a4a43988f3fe554fcf0`, and `9d9e315028c0ad0a403c5b033c83e842042818156fca9eb3bee28e0198f7a71a`. Entry/exit count and winner count exactly match D04.

**Economic result:** final one-account equity 123,261.52729696 USDT, annualized +23.23654%, 595/1,625 position wins (36.6154%), native daily-return Sharpe (252) 0.654930 and (365) 0.788208, native daily-close max drawdown -20.17079%. Versus D04 fixed quantity, sizing crosses the annual return gate through larger exposure, while the win rate stays far below the registered 55-65% range, Sharpe falls, and daily-close drawdown grows sharply from -2.69123%. This is a failed combined Goal candidate and a negative risk-adjusted tradeoff, not evidence of better entries. Retain both runs. The H01 native winner count stays 595 with or without those adjustments, as checked in `results/2026-10-08-h10-native-readback.json`.

Investigate source-fidelity of trade selection and exit semantics (especially four-hour range location, multi-timeframe resistance and staged target/stop events), with a small, registered source-chart acceptance set before another full economic replay. Keep 25 bps as a named exposure sensitivity, not the strategy baseline.

## Candidate H02: room to the next known daily opposing pivot

**Pre-registration before any H02 replay:** S04 and source cases C03/C04 show that Ronnie asks where the next resistance/support is before entry and avoids a range-middle trade with too little space; the frozen 2024 rules also state reward/risk at least 2. Test one deliberately limited, source-derived proxy without adding a new indicator: when an R-1u daily break creates a planned entry and structural stop, find the closest already confirmed **daily** pivot high above a long entry, or pivot low below a short entry. If one exists closer than the frozen 2R target, reject that signal and count it; if no opposing pivot is known, retain the baseline signal. Keep all other entry, stop, 2R target, expiry, sizing, data, venue and account settings identical to D04. The 2R threshold comes from the existing R-1u/source rule, not from a sweep over the viewed year. Evaluate fixed-size H02 against D04 first, then only if the selection mechanism is promising consider the separately registered 25-bps exposure sensitivity. This daily-only proxy does **not** prove four-hour/source-chart fidelity; check C03 and report mismatches. The replay is development evidence on an already-seen year, not an independent holdout.

**Small pilot, fidelity failure:** `/tmp/r1-rd-daily-room-pilot-BTC-ETH/` completed natively on the registered BTC/ETH subset before any 37-coin run. Its summary is retained in `results/2026-10-07-daily-room-h02-pilot.json` (SHA-256 `3561c1d1b715d88485fec528755326b59505309a647319f6faa460ccee19978a`); native positions and returns SHA-256 are `58e4d93ee171cb0727c2b65418e369fcaabb2aacff3dd515f0b4afea736b01fb` and `f331a7f73c654dd8766949d39621549f45b129bf5fc09665be24fbe65549b824`. The rule rejected 294/299 BTC and 542/543 ETH source signals, leaving five and one native positions respectively. Six closed, one profitable; annualized -0.05571% on the 100,000-USDT pilot account. The sample is too small for an economic estimate, and a nearest-pivot rule this indiscriminate does not match the author's choice of **key** four-hour/daily/weekly zones in C03. Stop H02 before a full economic sweep; changing a lookback or 2R threshold on the same year would be a new, ungrounded search. Retain the pilot and remove this failed optional rule from the active Strategy, while keeping the negative finding. A next candidate needs a source-chart-tested definition of which levels remain relevant after a breakout.

## Diagnostic D05: how many stopped trades first reached +1R?

**Question and read:** Before investing in a native partial-exit variant to raise the win rate, measure the opportunity in the clean D04 account without simulating new orders. `analyze_exits.py` joins each native closed position's entry fill to its linked native stop trigger, then scans the reused LAST five-minute catalog between native entry and exit timestamps. The strict measure excludes both boundary bars; the permissive measure includes both, even though its high/low may have occurred before entry or after exit. The code and `results/2026-10-07-exit-attribution-d05.json` reproduce this diagnostic; the JSON SHA-256 is `3291fc8f71209cc98a974011a8bb8dffb19d1481dbdf6478c7cfbdd08725d0d6`. The native positions and orders SHA-256 values are `31b67b2253a4f0515b0ec991be9db621fbddffbd424bb6299ba9db358462c246` and `6cbb2150adf8bea91292f6c336887049e5e2aa74a3887e7b3d655eb9922eb209`.

**Observed path:** 1,625 closed native positions include 595 positive, 1,027 closed by stop-market fills, 590 by limit target fills and eight by market fills. Of the stopped positions, 243 had a full intervening five-minute bar reach +1R relative to their actual entry fill and planned stop; including both ambiguous boundary bars raises that count to 257. At +1.5R the counts are 90 and 98. Thirty positions have no full intervening bar. These are price-path bounds, not alternate fills or net returns. The six stopped positions whose permissive boundary bar reaches +2R show why bar event order matters.

**Decision:** If *every* one of the 257 permissively observed +1R stopped positions became a positive trade and the trade set stayed fixed, the win rate would be at most `(595 + 257) / 1625 = 52.43%`, below the predeclared 55-65% interpretation of "near 60%". A +1R partial exit/breakeven rule alone therefore cannot close this Goal on the D04 trade set; costs and changed slot occupancy can only be evaluated by a new native run. R-1s first takes profit at 2R, so its staged exit likewise does not directly convert the 1,027 pre-target stop paths. The frozen 2018-22 X-2 study reported 61% for P1BE but lower total R; it used a different period and an hourly approximation, and does not overturn this current native-path diagnostic. Continue with source-chart-checked entry/context selection and separate native R-1s implementation acceptance, retaining D05 as a mechanism screen rather than an economic candidate.

## Candidate H03: confirmed four-hour box break and retest

**Registration before any H03 economic read:** S01, S03 and S04 repeatedly distinguish a range edge from its middle, demand a close through the level rather than a wick, and prefer the retest to chasing the break. C02-C04 are the source-chart acceptance cases. The frozen first-party G-2 lineage (`0725a7b3f89902e27cd421a18b4b879a13268534`, `research/ronnie/loop/LOG.md`) already specified a 60-bar four-hour box and a 12-bar resting retest; its reserve verdict later failed, so H03 has a weak prior, not a fresh discovery. H03 tests the more source-faithful signal timeframe with **native** four-hour aggregation and native orders; it does not claim to reproduce every discretionary confluence.

At each closed four-hour LAST bar, inspect only the preceding 60 completed, contiguous four-hour bars. A range requires high-minus-low between four and 15 times the already-known four-hour ATR(14), and at least two bars touching each edge within 0.25 ATR. A long break requires the prior close at or below the box high and the new close above it; shorts mirror at the box low. Wicks without a closing break arm nothing. Arm one native limit at the broken box edge for 12 four-hour bars. For a long, take the lowest wick of the upper-edge touch bars as the role-reversal zone's lower boundary and put the protective stop another 0.25 ATR below it; shorts mirror. Freeze a 2R target from the planned limit and stop, keep the 60-day maximum hold, one occupied slot per coin, and native maker/taker, MARK, funding, leverage and account handling. These numerical box and stop definitions come from the frozen G-2/R-1u research, not from a spoken exact Ronnie parameter; the target omits his conditional higher-timeframe ladder.

**Case predictions before PnL:** C02 wick-only penetration leaves no order. C03 an eventual *four-hour close* beyond a repeatedly tested local ETH box arms one limit at the broken edge; mere arrows and future resistance do not submit a trade, and this variant cannot claim to model the drawn higher-timeframe target ladder. C04 range-middle price without a closing break is idle. C01 a BTC range-edge support trade without a new close break is outside H03's coverage; retain that disagreement. C05 staged exits remain outside this entry-only variant. Use already completed bars at each decision; no screenshot price is used as a backtest fill.

**Planned evidence:** Reuse the registered 37-coin LAST/MARK/funding and current-Instrument assumption. The first ten input days build the 60-bar four-hour box; paired R-1u and H03 accounts begin order eligibility at 2025-10-17 00:00 UTC, both from 100,000 USDT with no prior trades, and end at 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC. Annualize against that eligible interval. First run BTC/ETH for aggregation, signal and order integrity, independent of pilot PnL; if source-case behavior and native protection hold, run all 37 coins whether pilot PnL is positive or negative. Compare against a same-window R-1u replay with identical sizing, then separately the previously registered 25-bp risk sizing only if the fixed-size mechanism is interpretable. Preserve every attempt and denial/rejection, Sharpe, drawdown and limitations. The entire year is exposed development data; no positive number from H03 can close the Goal without later forward evidence.

**Source geometry and pilot:** `test_box_signal.py` checks the registered 60-bar continuity, two edge touches, four-to-15 ATR width, close rather than wick break, structural stop outside the touched edge wicks, and idle middle. These are synthetic geometries derived from C02-C04, not a pixel-level reproduction of the spot video. C01 and the conditional target ladder remain explicitly uncovered. The BTC/ETH H03 native run `/tmp/r1-rd-box-h03-pilot-BTC-ETH/` yielded 51 source signals, 35 closed positions, 14 positive and zero denied/rejected orders; its same-window R-1u comparison `/tmp/r1-rd-h03-paired-r1u-pilot-BTC-ETH/` yielded 110 closed, 38 positive and zero denied/rejected. The pilot summaries are retained in `results/2026-10-07-btc-eth-box-h03-pilot.json` and `results/2026-10-07-btc-eth-h03-paired-r1u-pilot.json`. Pilot PnL was not used to choose a threshold or whether to run the 37 coins.

**Full paired native replay:** H03 `/tmp/r1-rd-box-h03-37/` and R-1u `/tmp/r1-rd-h03-paired-r1u-37/` used the same 37 coins, 100,000-USDT shared margin account definition, fixed per-coin quantity, input 2025-10-07 to 2026-10-07, order eligibility from 2025-10-17, and end 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC. Each streamed 3,893,214 LAST and MARK bars and 42,779 settled funding records. The registered per-coin minute and daily catalog tree hashes plus quantity CSV were rechecked against `results/2026-10-07-input-identity.json`: 74 catalog trees and the quantity hash matched. Full native summaries are retained in `results/2026-10-07-37coin-box-h03.json` (SHA-256 `13480d33e63c6a9d3ec7991cee07d8be6752de97438e3edb1df8228c5ff36f03`) and `results/2026-10-07-37coin-h03-paired-r1u.json` (SHA-256 `be38d87c25c4d2caec1f34103d85216fcdda0c0490a7f1d3e3ec58d972060bc5`). Their native positions SHA-256 values are `b16f60a65c00017cf51a7428da818c3eb3bbb6cc787895fe32430b6c566ab8fe` and `c127554de3ad52771fef7c1dcc39e8d4d007e37610cfeb8b31030d5f779f5810`; native returns SHA-256 values are `534ec15f20159e23267795a736209541675a471780547cbf12e0b048c18f6734` and `fe4d898b31583d47a4f1860d193cf37ec9d225e418c3c66b929699e4552f8dc4`.

Both comparison files have identical sampled numbers because the paired native return series are byte-identical; the later file binds the exact-source summary hashes. This read was added after seeing point estimates; it is not a preregistered or multiplicity-adjusted qualification result. H03 trades less and has a better observed drawdown, but neither the >20% annual return nor the 55-65% win-rate screen passes. Keep it as a source-derived negative/diagnostic candidate, do not tune box length, touch count, stop or sizing on this year. The 25-bp sensitivity would not repair the win-rate failure and is not selected for another full replay. A future source mechanism must explain which **particular** four-hour/daily/weekly zone is relevant and how direction/range context is chosen, rather than merely detecting any box breakout. Minute-sampled drawdown and independent forward evidence remain outstanding.

## Source access S06: support reaction versus failed support

**Selection registered before media acquisition:** The frozen first-party `research/ronnie/tv/video_ideas.jsonl.gz` at `0725a7b3f89902e27cd421a18b4b879a13268534` identifies Ronnie_Dong's original [2024-11-19 DOGE support video](https://video-ideas.tradingview.com/1/1416280-f1hAXIls3Y2mrc7q.mp4) (`MZNspcsN`) and [2025-03-10 ETH support-loss video](https://video-ideas.tradingview.com/1/1416280-RDuLm44RB_k-HCh2.mp4) (`Zlp57IP3`). Their titles suggest contrasting situations, but are **not** evidence of what he said or drew. Inspect when he regards support as valid, what confirms a break, whether an entry is a resting limit or awaits a close/retest, the structural stop, target room and direction context. Where audio or pixels do not establish a rule, mark it unknown. Do not use these old spot-chart prices as Binance perpetual fills or score a new strategy merely because a chart looks favorable. Add source-case predictions before any annual PnL read for a proposed variant.

**Acquisition and inspected source:** Both public MP4s succeeded through the current `video-note-mcp --quality fast` CLI. The DOGE complete note is `/Users/vx/.local/share/bilibili-note-mcp/notes/note-8b5dd062e3f14ba5aa9dd460938a60fa/note.md` (SHA-256 `0fda7e4be0f7e725b5297efea44d22ee759250cc10fc769c3a1b68b1cd09489e`, 21 original screenshots); the ETH note is `/Users/vx/.local/share/bilibili-note-mcp/notes/note-e49045be678d4613b06c1a8bb7b3889a/note.md` (SHA-256 `08f6b5860fa49d7037053e35aaadba1cddd1294372f7c26e7ddd5971100a9f1d`, 11 screenshots). The frozen metadata archive blob is Git `999e392946dcd4e44e3a24313b7d91eed49fadd7`; the generic note labels author/date unknown, so source identity/date here come from that archive, not from the note generator. Transcription was fast, with no independent audio review; unclear numbers below are not strategy parameters.

**DOGE, 00:00-02:31 and 03:13-04:02:** The speaker describes a previously resting buy near 0.34 after support was tested and a later 15% rise. He distinguishes an already profitable spot/low-average holding from a **new** leveraged chase after the rise. For a new long, the valid stop would still be below the support band, making the current contract entry unattractive to him. At 02:22, the original daily DOGE frame `/Users/vx/.local/share/bilibili-note-mcp/notes/note-8b5dd062e3f14ba5aa9dd460938a60fa/images/source-1-5.png` (SHA-256 `711adf4f534dc849a330d3c53b1c8ae89a5d32446c90795aace75f9a879d3a4d`) shows the marked band below the current price. At 03:31-04:02 he describes BTC as a bullish four-hour consolidation and warns against taking the upper resistance alone as a reason to short. This distinguishes *position continuation* from *fresh entry* and combines higher-timeframe direction with local range location. He does not state a numerical maximum stop distance or a universal formula for selecting the band.

**ETH, 00:24-03:51 and BTC 04:00-04:40:** The speaker sees a strong weekly bearish candle, yet regards the relevant weekly/daily role-reversal support as still potentially intact. On the daily chart, a wick tested below about 2,000 and returned; the first bearish day did not close decisively through the support. He waits for the next completed daily candle to extend the break; a bullish reclaim would instead leave support valid. At 02:59, the original daily ETH frame `/Users/vx/.local/share/bilibili-note-mcp/notes/note-e49045be678d4613b06c1a8bb7b3889a/images/source-1-3.png` (SHA-256 `2ddf33ad9a6031534941081d73f25b0a2c38b02096a3d1ef5e63ab0ed919025a`) shows the horizontal support band and descending line while price is near the band. He explicitly warns against an immediate short at this ambiguous support. The BTC section separately favors a pullback to a key support within a larger bullish direction rather than arbitrary points in the four-hour range. No measured body ratio, band width, or exact futures trigger price is established by the audio/frames.

**Decision:** Add source cases C07-C09. S06 reinforces that R-1u's daily pivot break and H03's generic box are only partial encodings: neither carries the complete chart-selected key zone, position history, timeframe-specific stop and direction context. It does **not** prove that the native implementation misprices an order, that all shorts should be filtered, or that a new numeric threshold will improve the exposed year. No H04 economic variant is registered from these two examples. The next entry/context candidate needs an explicit ex-ante definition that passes C01/C07-C09 and acknowledges contrary cases (for example shorting a major upper zone in a bullish market); only then should it receive a new native paired run. The old 37-coin economic results are unchanged.

## Diagnostic D06: H03 stopped trades that first reached +1R

**Question registered before reading the result:** C05 directly shows a first partial target and later protection change, while H03 leaves its entire position until a fixed 2R target or stop. On the exact-source H03 run, join each native closed position's entry fill to its linked native stop trigger, then scan the same five-minute LAST bars between entry and exit with `analyze_exits.py`. Count stopped positions whose favorable path reached +0.5R, +1R, +1.5R and +2R, using strict interior bars and a permissive version including ambiguous entry/exit bars. As with D05, `(native positive positions + stopped positions reaching +1R) / all native closed positions` is an intentionally optimistic same-trade-set win-rate bound, not a new partial-fill result. If even the permissive bound is below the declared 55% lower screen, do not run an H03-only +1R staged-exit sweep; source selection/direction and a complete native R-1s implementation remain separate work. If it reaches 55%, this only justifies considering a **pre-registered** full native partial-exit replay with updated slot/account paths, not a performance claim. Preserve the diagnostic output and source hashes regardless of sign. The historical year is already exposed.

**Observed native-path bound:** `results/2026-10-07-h03-exit-attribution-d06.json` (SHA-256 `af4b4f1eb2489b8fd307f624ea7cc55ad4194a73d2586ae30c3da2b4d5b8f221`) is tied to exact-source H03 positions SHA-256 `e6c3c869fe2b2fdb47b9a56c5c0404c7b980bbc2279b67da13f5d9fa5aa55d82` and orders SHA-256 `fcd27d2b742c2ad3682899d395784c18411defde4f4ae54dc14ec6c6346f1dc2`. Of 555 native closed positions, 209 were positive, 344 closed by stop, 205 by limit target and six by market. Among stopped positions, 80 reached +1R in a full interior five-minute bar, or 87 with ambiguous boundary bars included; at +2R, none did so strictly and four did so permissively. Twelve positions have no full interior bar. Even converting **all** 87 permissive +1R stopped positions into wins on the identical trade set gives only 296/555 = **53.33%**, below the declared 55% lower screen. This is an upper opportunity count, not an executable staged-exit net result. Under the predeclared decision rule, stop the H03-only +1R exit branch; do not select the observed +0.5R count as a new target from this same year's result. Native R-1s remains a product acceptance requirement and can be built for its own semantics, but this diagnostic supplies no H03 Goal gate argument. Prioritize source-faithful selection and direction/context, with new source evidence or delayed forward observation before claiming qualification.

## Candidate H04: native R-1s staged exit on the frozen daily signal

**Registration before implementation and any economic read:** The user chose the full R-1s staged-exit scope and the **farther** remainder interpretation; `docs/scenarios/research.{md,zh.md}` is the current scenario contract. Retain the R-1u daily pivot signal, ten-day resting limit, structural initial stop, 60-day maximum hold, one occupied slot per coin, same current-Instrument assumption and same native data/account/cost inputs as the exact-source R-1u pair. At the signal close, freeze the first target as R-1u's planned-entry 2R price. The source R-F impulse uses the last already-confirmed order-3 opposite pivot A before the break and the most extreme price B from A through that closed break bar; retain that impulse at the signal. After entry, set the second half's target to the farther in-profit of the frozen 2R target and actual average entry plus one signed impulse length. A closer/equal impulse target leaves the second target at 2R. This differs from the older prototype's *nearer* second target and must be reported as a separately named variant, never a silent R-1u mutation.

Split the **actual filled quantity** with the native Instrument increment: first half rounded down, remainder receives the residue. Reject a known-at-submission invalid split with a named count; a partially filled entry must keep its actual position protected and surface any split impossibility. The native execution/Portfolio facts own every fill, fee, funding charge, open quantity and realized PnL. Maintain full-size initial stop protection until native first-target fill feedback; then update the remaining stop quantity and trigger to the actual average entry using native order APIs. A touch, accepted target order or submitted modification is not a fill/updated protection. A stop before first target cancels both targets; a later stop or time exit closes only remaining native quantity. A partially filled first target leaves full remaining protection and does not mark its leg complete until its registered first-half quantity has actually filled. Preserve order/position association and event-frontier diagnostics; no toy fills, parallel ledger or synthetic account.

**Case and economic gates:** Before annual PnL, show with small native cases that odd Instrument quantities cannot create zero/overlapping legs, entry/first-target partial fills preserve exact protection, stop versus TP collision follows the declared native OHLC path, a successful first target moves only the remainder stop, and a 60-day exit frees the slot only when native position is flat. C05 must be represented as a staged *plan*, not a simultaneous later reversal; C03's conditional higher-timeframe target is only approximated by the registered impulse and must retain that disagreement. Run a BTC/ETH native pilot for order integrity, then the same-window 37-coin shared-account native paired R-1s/R-1u comparison if integrity passes, independent of pilot PnL sign. Record source/runner bytes, Catalog digests, native orders/fills/positions/account/returns, rejection and protection checks, final equity, annualized return, position-level win rate, Sharpe (252/365) and labeled drawdown. The exposed year gives development evidence only; test multiplicity and later forward observation are still needed. D05's same-trade-set upper bound already shows that stage management alone has no credible argument for reaching 55% wins in the current R-1u trade population, so H04 is not selected or sized to force the Goal thresholds.

**Execution-detail addendum before H04 economics:** Implement H04 as a separate native Strategy class sharing the unchanged daily-pivot signal machinery. A missing confirmed opposite pivot leaves the far target at fixed 2R and increments a named count; it does not silently remove the R-1u entry signal. A known-at-submission quantity below two native steps is skipped with a named count. After the first partial entry fill, request cancellation of the unfilled remainder while the native OTO stop protects the actual open quantity; repeated entry fills before cancellation must resync the stop. Only a terminal entry order with accepted protection matching the actual native position may create first and last reduce-only target orders. If the terminal actual quantity cannot form two positive native-step legs, close it through a native order and count the case. Once targets exist, each native exit fill resynchronizes stop leaves with the native position; the first target's full native fill activates the breakeven trigger request, while a partial first-target fill changes quantity only. Slot reuse waits for native flat position **and** old exits to become terminal. A protection rejection or orphaned exposure fails order-integrity acceptance, regardless of PnL. Native callback/command latency and same-bar ambiguity remain explicit D11-D13 limits; no fabricated OHLC tick path is inserted into the annual replay.

## Diagnostic D11: native staged-order amendment timing before H04 annual replay

**Question:** Can a single native OTO entry protect the entire actual position, then let two native reduce-only targets exit separate quantities while a first-target fill moves only the remaining stop to actual entry? Does a bar covering both the first target and old stop respect the requested amendment before the later stop? This is an H04 execution gate, not an economic candidate. Use the current BTC Instrument from the reused Catalog and synthetic five-minute LAST/MARK prices; no exchange credentials, new market data or performance calculation.

**Probe and observed events:** `probe_staged_orders.py` SHA-256 `0eba7b2621d4e71238cbdcbddfba55a9ee9c0e205c523809fb88f9b45889b3b2` runs a native BacktestEngine with OTO limit entry 0.003 BTC, linked full-size initial stop, and two separately submitted reduce-only native targets after the entry fill. The prices are arbitrary mechanics fixtures, not registered H04 signal prices. Three assertion-checked native event paths are saved as `results/2026-10-07-h04-probe-{normal,stop-first,collision}.json`, SHA-256 respectively `f32b2c30fca1c6b780f533ec376a5155ae087fb7adc730915537a9c6bee94cf5`, `a4ea270227862f4a844adf59c6ea1c42adacf8e4e3f48d3c76f6287bcc584711`, `4ec3eab0e463f8a05713d14f0c0de507c87aa56966c94ea15d531dd80183a129`. All cases close exactly one native position. In the normal path, first-target fill 0.001 BTC leaves 0.002, and the stop eventually updates to quantity 0.002 and the actual 85,269.90 entry price; the last target fills 0.002 and the stop cancels. In stop-first, the 0.003 stop fills and both targets cancel. In collision, the first 0.001 target fills, a native stop update event carries quantity 0.002 but no new trigger price, and the 0.002 stop then fills with its original 85,100 trigger; the final target cancels. Native callbacks and OHLC event ordering, not a toy account, produced these facts. The initial probe fixture used bar volume with the wrong Instrument precision and therefore produced no fills; it was repaired to use `instrument.make_qty(100)` before these recorded paths. This is a fixture correction, not an R-1 strategy change.

**Decision:** H04 has **not** passed its order-integrity gate. A Strategy `modify_order(quantity=..., trigger_price=...)` call after the first target is not proof that the new trigger has become effective for a collision in the same execution bar. Do not run or interpret a 37-coin H04 economic replay yet. Next isolate whether native event sequencing, amendment representation, or five-minute bar granularity causes the old-trigger fill; verify a solution with a collision, odd quantity and partial-fill cases, then run the prescribed BTC/ETH pilot and paired 37-coin replay. Preserve the registered exit rules and use native orders/Portfolio; do not replace the simulator or silently score a weaker staged variant.

## Diagnostic D12: command-queue boundary in the same-bar collision

**Question registered before the read:** D11 shows a same-bar first-target/old-stop collision despite a Strategy stop-amend request. The native matching engine's five-minute bar processor generates synthetic open/high/low/close trade ticks in one `process_bar` call, while the BacktestEngine drains Strategy trading commands around data processing. Run the identical synthetic collision with `SimulatedExchange.use_message_queue=false`, leaving Instrument, bar path, orders and Strategy callback unchanged. If the stop still fills at the old trigger, queue configuration alone cannot close the H04 semantics gap; inspect event delivery across the entire bar and avoid changing annual run configuration. If the new trigger becomes effective within the bar, determine whether synchronous processing is a supported and comparable native setting before considering it, and test entry/partial-fill/latency consequences. No economic results or variant selection follow from this diagnostic.

**Observed result:** The `use_message_queue=false` collision attempt failed before any entry fill: native execution recursively reentered its `ExecutionEngine` while the initial OTO list was being accepted and raised `RefCell already borrowed` at `crates/core/src/shared.rs:64`. The backtrace passes through `SimulatedExchange::send`, `process_trading_command`, matching-engine `accept_order`, then the execution-engine order-event handler while the same engine remains borrowed. A repeat with `RUST_BACKTRACE=1` confirmed the same stack. This configuration is not a viable H04 shortcut in the current runtime; no collision trade or economic result came from it. Keep the standard queued configuration and test event delivery across distinct native input events.

## Diagnostic D13: native trade-event separation of a collision bar

**Question registered before the read:** D11's ordinary queued five-minute bar processes high and low inside one exchange `process_bar` call. Replace only that collision bar with four native `TradeTick` events representing the same open, high, low and close in chronological order inside the five-minute interval; keep the first two and last two bars, native Instrument, mark updates, Strategy, OTO entry, stop and targets unchanged. If the stop modification becomes effective between high and low, the D11 boundary is bar event granularity rather than an unsupported stop amendment. If it still fills at the old trigger, investigate command/event ordering independent of bar granularity. This synthetic micro-case is not market data and cannot justify filling a historical bar with invented ticks or estimating performance.

**Observed native event read:** `probe_staged_orders.py` SHA-256 `a197946da11a562877227688f10a099efdd25cc0c31a73fc92e0d77214842525` produced `results/2026-10-07-h04-probe-collision-ticks.json` SHA-256 `7f8bac2b0e951f7717516e508d5a672daca215181c404e75db07deccca3fcc28`. First target filled 0.001 BTC at 85,500 on the synthetic high-side TradeTick; before the later low-side tick, native `OrderUpdated` carried both stop quantity 0.002 and new trigger 85,269.90. The remaining stop then filled 0.002 BTC at **84,900**, because the next synthetic tick jumped through that trigger. Both bar and separated-tick cases closed one native position and canceled the unused last target. The bar case had no trigger-price update before its low and filled the stop at its old 85,100 trigger. Thus the event boundary changes whether the amendment takes effect before the subsequent price observation; it does not guarantee a breakeven execution price. This is a controlled mechanics contrast, not a historical trade reconstruction or an improvement in PnL.

**Decision:** Keep native message queuing. To qualify H04's staged protection, inspect *both* accepted trigger changes and native stop fill prices, and identify real historical first-target/stop collision intervals. Never synthesize four OHLC TradeTicks into the annual Catalog as if their timestamps and path were observed. Continue the native Strategy implementation with explicit collision diagnostics; evaluate whether bounded public historical futures trades can resolve observed ambiguous intervals, or report a conservative unresolved interval if not. Complete odd-quantity, partial-fill, and 60-day slot cases before the BTC/ETH pilot and full 37-coin economic comparison.

## Diagnostic D15: H04 actual Strategy lifecycle and invalid-price admission

**Registered before the 37-coin corrective replay:** The separate native `R1StagedStrategy` shares R-1u's daily-pivot signal and account inputs, freezes the source impulse at the closed break, and uses a native OTO entry/stop plus two native reduce-only targets. Six synthetic native cases are required before economic reading: ordinary odd-step split, a one-step known split skip, a partially filled entry whose actual one-step position is protected then closed because it cannot split, a first target that fills in two native trades while the stop first changes quantity only and then moves trigger after full leg completion, a same-bar target/stop collision that preserves the actual old-trigger outcome, and a native 60-day exit followed by old-order cleanup and fresh slot use. ## Diagnostic D18: two-scale intact support zones at source time

**Definition registered before any D18 level read:** Reuse only the same native historical daily bars and frozen publication cutoffs as D16/D17. Confirm order-3 and order-20 pivot highs/lows only after their right-side bars have closed. A pivot high contributes its candle's body-top-to-wick-high zone; a pivot low contributes wick-low-to-body-bottom zone, capped to one already-known daily ATR **at confirmation** from the pivot's wick as in R-1u. A swing-low zone becomes support on its first completed close above the zone's upper edge; a former-high zone becomes support only after a **large-bodied completed close** above its upper edge. From confirmation for lows and from support activation for former highs, a large-bodied completed close below the lower edge permanently invalidates that instance; a wick alone does not. At the source time only active zones whose upper edge is **strictly below** the last completed daily close qualify. A candidate is *key* if it is from order 20 or its price interval overlaps an active order-3 support zone from a distinct pivot day; when several remain, take the closest key zone below current closed price, with older confirmed time breaking ties. Report selected zone's pivot source, interval, confirmation and invalidation state, the nearest non-key alternative, active candidates and rejected counts so the choice is auditable. This is a source geometry probe, not a new entry signal or target selector; it does not trade. The selection is frozen for this read: do not change pivot orders, ATR cap, overlap, body criterion or ranking after inspecting levels.

**Source-case predictions and decision gate:** At C07 DOGE the selected support should correspond to the previously rested area near 0.34, at C10 AAVE to the lower area near 195, and at C08 ETH to the still-ambiguous area near 2,000; compare on the video's spot chart with a tolerance of **one prior completed daily ATR**, reporting the actual futures ATR and raw difference. C01's four-hour trendline/edge and C03's conditional four-hour break/target ladder are out of this daily selector's coverage and remain explicit disagreements. Passing all three location checks would justify only a native Strategy source-case implementation attempt; a failure keeps H05 unregistered and directs new source work toward zone importance or timeframe choice. No annual return, win rate, fill or future candle is read in this diagnostic.

**Observed result:** `audit_source_support_zones.py` SHA-256 `ae3d0c3093cbfdaa7cbbd15db9b198488bc2221720a5a0e15e288beb542227cc` read the same native daily Catalog trees and frozen source publication cutoffs. ## Mechanism continuation after D18: H05 source branches

D18 rejected one causal *horizontal* support selector: order-3/order-20 confirmed pivot zones, permanent large-body close invalidation and nearest-key ranking. D20 rejected a gross source-clock/price mismatch as the sole explanation at its one-percent descriptive threshold. Neither result rejects every structural-support interpretation. S09 supports the existence of rising-line and structural-stop reasoning but supplies no line anchors or reclaim rule. Therefore the parent H05 mechanism stays open while the D18 implementation stays failed and no annual economic variant is registered.

- **H05a, dynamic support:** An independently sourced rising line or line/horizontal confluence, constructed only from pivots known before a decision, might explain an AAVE-like conditional lower long without reviving the invalidated horizontal zone. Before coding it, seek an author-owned case that identifies how the line is drawn and when a touch, close, or failed break changes the plan. Freeze line anchors, decision time and predictions on AAVE plus an independent chart case; a candidate that needs a future pivot, misses the independent case, or contradicts the author's stated action fails this branch. S09 alone does not meet this gate.
- **H05b, new support after reclaim:** A completed break may be followed by a later *new* structure and a distinct conditional long. Look for an author-owned break/reclaim example with order of events, accepted close, new support/stop and decision time. Predict the state before and after reclaim on that case and on AAVE without using post-decision bars. If the speaker merely keeps an old drawing or gives no reclaim condition, do not infer a mechanical reactivation rule. The D18 zone remains invalid throughout.

These are competing source explanations, not two profitable strategies or permission to sweep line tolerances on the exposed annual returns. The next source read should state which question it tests before acquisition, keep negative results, and only then propose one frozen native Strategy and source-case gate. A source failure narrows that child, not automatically the parent; close H05 only with a recorded mechanism-level reason or a declared research bound.

## Candidate H06: frozen strong four-hour trend-line break, native port

**Registration before a new native H06 result:** The pre-existing Git `0725a7b3f89902e27cd421a18b4b879a13268534`, `research/ronnie/combo/candidates/trendline_break_strong.py`, is an already frozen, externally evaluated research candidate. Its `combo/holdout_result.txt` reports 1,653 trades and average R minus matched control +0.136 with a 98.3% coin-then-signal interval [+0.017,+0.252]; the source's simplified fill/fee and independent-trade model is **not** a Nautilus account result. Its later descriptive toy portfolio uses 17 coins and reports 2025 +1%, 2026 -17%, with all those coins already exposed; neither old result is independent evidence for the present 2025-10 to 2026-10 native 37-coin window. H06 is selected because S11 independently shows a strong completed line break versus a weak apparent one, not because a new annual result was searched.

**Frozen rule to port:** On each *completed four-hour* LAST candle, confirm order-8 high/low pivots only after eight right-side candles close. A long uses the latest two confirmed falling pivot highs; a short uses the latest two confirmed rising pivot lows. Anchor indices must be at least six bars apart and the second anchor no more than 60 bars old. The first close through the projected line kills that anchor pair whether strong or weak; signal only if all previous closes since the second anchor stayed on the old side and this first crossing candle has a direction-aligned body at least 1.0 times the ATR(14) known **before** that candle, with its close in the outer 30% of its own range. Entry is a native market order after that completed candle, with native attached stop at the candle's opposite extreme, limit target 2R from the signal close, and a time exit after 30 completed four-hour bars. This faithfully retains the old candidate's assumptions; the line may miss Ronnie's hand drawing, and FTM's later strong candle after an earlier weak crossing would require a separately registered child, not an unreported H06 repair. No new strength threshold, pivot order, line tolerance or R target will be selected from this year's outcomes.

**Pre-result gates and replay:** First test causal pivot confirmation, first-crossing consumption, strong versus weak candle decisions, and native market bracket lifecycle. C13 gives the categorical MATIC strong-break versus FTM wait contrast; because the source charts are spot, no unique line-anchor algorithm was spoken, and MATIC discusses *already held* longs, C13 cannot validate H06's numerical anchors or a new futures market order. Preserve those source mismatches rather than calling the whole Ronnie method reproduced. Use the existing 37 Binance perpetual five-minute LAST/MARK/funding Catalogs and input identity `results/2026-10-07-input-identity.json`, the same fixed quantity manifest, one native 100,000-USDT margin account, 1x default leverage, order eligibility 2025-10-17 00:00 UTC and end 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC as H03. Run BTC/ETH first for execution integrity, then all 37 regardless of pilot PnL if the gate passes; preserve failures and all native reports. Pair with the same-window R-1u account, checking exact input and configuration parity before attributing any difference. Report native net annual return, closed-position win rate, 365-day Sharpe, fee/funding-bearing account equity, daily and minute drawdown availability, denials/rejections, trade count and source mismatch. All results on this year are exposed development evidence; an attractive point estimate cannot close the Goal without multiplicity-aware uncertainty and later forward evidence. A source or native execution failure stops the economic read until repaired without changing the rule silently.

**H06 implementation and pre-result checks:** `trendline_strategy.py` SHA-256 `c71dff66060ad5338acce663d3da010e040f0486419f9eec269f26193f147142` ports the frozen signal to a Nautilus Strategy, with native market OTO entry, stop, limit target, account and time exit; `run_portfolio.py` SHA-256 `f2889b1bb46078d0e285080d6ecca1bb2b4100e9e3a2354582d535f249f36896` selects it in the existing shared-account runner. An initial read-only parity check grouped five-minute bars by interval end instead of their close-minus-1-ms event timestamp, producing 47 bars in the first four-hour bucket and one in a spurious final bucket; correcting that audit grouping gave 48 bars per complete bucket. A first BTC/ETH pilot then exposed a causal state discrepancy: the frozen source consumes a crossed line pair even before signal bar 30, while the initial port skipped the whole check. The port was fixed without changing the registered rule; the initial pilot report was overwritten before a durable copy and must not be treated as a retained economic comparison. `results/2026-10-08-h06-37-signal-parity.json` SHA-256 `953f66e6c55dd4d78471a538b564bd439b9eece5204e277e15756d03362ed95e` compares the frozen source SHA-256 `090142d1e7ee758d37fca11be24c1c8c95589ae3f1b413c55c0929ef79c4d202` with the corrected Strategy helper on all 37 reused LAST catalogs. Every completed four-hour signal matched in time, side, stop and target to eight decimals; the partial final four-hour bucket was excluded from both signal sets. This is signal parity, not order or economic parity. The corrected BTC/ETH pilot `/tmp/r1-rd-h06-pilot-BTC-ETH/` is retained with `results/2026-10-08-h06-btc-eth-integrity.json` SHA-256 `dd61611ef3851377348d131d081eae0abf747c312e053f42623cffdd3d75c6f6`: 31 native entry brackets, 30 closed positions, one open with accepted full-size stop and target, eight native time exits, zero denial or rejection. ## Candidate H08: rising-line support continuation, exploratory native long

**Registration before implementation and any H08 economic read:** H06 entered on the first strong *break across* a projected line; it lost on the exposed year. S16 BTC/LINK instead discuss tests of an intact rising support line, S09 IMX describes a support-area long with a structural stop and prior high/channel edge as target, and C10 AAVE rejects a fresh long far above support. H08 tests this different mechanism, not a threshold repair of H06 or a claim of exact Ronnie replication. The frozen positive source categories are S16 BTC's completed line test and LINK's current support test, plus C11 IMX's support-order intent; the negative categories are S16 ETH's already broken hourly line and C10 AAVE's late entry near an overhead obstacle. These cases predict *support location versus chase and intact versus broken line*, not a precise numeric signal or a contemporaneous new order. Source fidelity remains partial even if the mechanism screen is profitable.

**One frozen researcher encoding:** On each completed four-hour LAST candle, use only order-8 pivot lows confirmed eight bars later, as already frozen in H06. The latest two confirmed lows must rise, be at least six four-hour bars apart, and the second must be no older than 60 bars; their projection defines the support line, and any earlier close through that pair's projected line kills the pair. Require the previous completed candle's close and low to be above the line plus a 0.25 times *prior* ATR(14) touch band, then the current candle to touch within that band, close above the line and above its open. This first contact after an above-band bar is an observed rejection, not a future arrow. The planned stop is below the lesser of the current low and projected support by 0.25 prior ATR, reusing R-1u's structural buffer; the target is the maximum high of the preceding 60 *completed* four-hour bars, reusing H03's range window and S09/S16's prior-high obstacle concept. Do not enter if that target is at or below the signal close, if the close already cleared it, or if the planned distance to target is less than the stop distance (one planned R); the one-R floor is an explicit researcher risk/room assumption, not an uttered Ronnie threshold. Place one native market OTO long after the completed signal, with its native stop and limit target, and time-exit after 30 further completed four-hour bars as in H06. Keep one live position per coin, the registered fixed quantities and same one-account cost model. No bearish mirror, stop tightening, retest wait, new coin selection, or parameter sweep is admitted in H08. Record signal counts and skip reasons.

**Pre-result gates and replay:** Unit geometry must show causal pivot confirmation, an above-band previous bar, a touch/rejection signal, a broken-line rejection, the prior-high one-R room check, and no input-gap or lookahead acceptance. A native synthetic order lifecycle must show accepted market entry with full-size stop/target and a terminal close. Compare the source-case decision categories above and preserve where source charts cannot prove the numeric anchors or market entry. If these gates pass, run BTC/ETH first for native integrity and then all 37 coins regardless of pilot PnL, using the same registered 2025-10-07 to 2026-10-07 five-minute LAST/MARK/funding Catalogs, daily Catalog identity, quantity manifest, 100,000-USDT native margin account, 1x leverage, 2025-10-17 00:00 UTC order eligibility and 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC end as H06. ## Candidate H08b: one-bar resting limit at a known rising-line band

**Registration before implementation and H08b results:** S18 proves a rising LINK line existed the day before the current test, while S19 LTC independently separates an immediate breakout entry from waiting at previously drawn support. Neither video establishes a numeric anchor algorithm, an actual resting limit, a new fill or a stop; H08b is an explicitly exploratory *researcher* encoding of the timing mechanism, not an exact Ronnie order reconstruction or a repair of H08a's failed broad-line selector. Reuse H08's already frozen latest two order-8 confirmed four-hour pivot lows, minimum six-bar span, maximum 60-bar second-anchor age, close-break invalidation, 0.25 prior ATR band, prior 60-bar high target, one planned R room and 30-bar maximum hold. No new coin, anchor selection, touch width, target scan, bearish mirror or PnL-driven parameter search is admitted.

**One causal order rule:** At each completed four-hour LAST bar after warmup and eligibility, require the latest rising pair to be intact and that bar's *low and close* both remain strictly above its projected line plus 0.25 times the prior ATR(14). Using only this completed bar, project the line one four-hour bar forward and set a BUY limit at that projection plus the same already known 0.25 prior ATR band. Reject a projected limit at or above the decision close, because that would be immediately marketable rather than wait for support. The planned stop is the projected line minus 0.25 prior ATR; the planned target is the highest high of the 60 completed four-hour bars ending at the decision. Require target above limit by at least limit-minus-stop and positive rounded prices. Submit a native Nautilus bracket only after the decision bar; the entry expires immediately before the next four-hour boundary if unfilled. At the next completed bar, recompute only after the prior entry is terminal; if the pair closed through its line, mark it dead. One live entry/position per coin; no order is retroactively placed into the bar that disclosed a test. On fill, native stop/target protect full size and a native market time exit closes any remainder after 30 further completed four-hour bars measured from fill. Keep H06/H08 cost, quantity, account and data assumptions. This compares a pre-touch resting path with H08's after-green-close market path; their orders are mutually exclusive variants.

**Pre-result gates and replay:** C16 LINK predicts a causal order intent before the July 24 current test from a line already known at the July 24 00:00 UTC completed boundary, and its next four-hour LAST low should cross the frozen upper touch band; this is an intent/fill-model check, not proof of a Ronnie order. S16 BTC's H08 latest-pair break must suppress a false intact-support order near the observed rebound; ETH's broken hourly line cannot be promoted to a four-hour reversal or a new held order. Synthetic geometry must verify pivot confirmation, no current-bar hindsight, one-bar expiry, close-break invalidation, room check and no duplicate live entries; the native lifecycle must show an accepted limit, protected fill and terminal exits, plus an unfilled expiry case. If these gates fail, retain the failure without reading annual economics. If they pass, run BTC/ETH native integrity first, then all 37 on the same D21-corrected 2025-10-07 to 2026-10-07 five-minute LAST/MARK/funding Catalogs and single 100,000-USDT margin account as H06/H08, regardless of pilot PnL. Retain exact source and input hashes, orders/fills/protection, fees/funding, return, win, payoff, Sharpe, drawdown and per-coin contributions. The whole year remains exposed development data, so even a favorable annual point estimate cannot alone qualify the Goal; compare with H06 on the same account and keep a later forward gate.

**Source-period geometry gate:** `audit_resting_line_source_gate.py` reads the frozen corrected H08 source-geometry artifact SHA-256 `eba4ca34c4ad8a0fd558c8db136055d286edaa5afef43b428d77924400959239` and writes `results/2026-10-08-h08b-source-gate.json` SHA-256 `b08e49df4bd27befca46b4dcb2c3985f955b6ad5ae9ce9c599e1b48f25a33de8`. ## Candidate H10: prospective four-hour range-edge order, direct economic test

**Registered before implementation or H10 source-date/economic read:** S01's BTC four-hour range plan prefers its lower support/confluence rather than its middle; S04 describes the upper range resistance as a target for a held long and a possible *later* short zone, while S03 warns against buying an extended candle. H03's already frozen 60-bar four-hour box captured a break/retest path but won only 37.66% of its 555 closed positions; it did not test *range-edge mean reversion*. H10 is one distinct, explicitly researcher-encoded conditional path: submit a native limit **before a future edge touch**, seeking the range middle as the first known obstacle. The source does not give a unique box lookback, ATR band, exact stop, target, resting duration or author exchange order; H10 cannot be labeled a literal Ronnie order. It asks whether switching the entry location and direction class on the same 37-coin perpetual account can move both return and win rate, not whether a tiny H06 line adjustment rescues a failed rule.

At each completed four-hour LAST bar, use only the **preceding 60 contiguous completed four-hour bars**, the ATR(14) value known *before* the current bar, and H03's frozen box test: box width from four through 15 prior ATR, with at least two prior candles within 0.25 prior ATR of **each** high and low edge. If the current close lies strictly above `box_low + 0.25*ATR` and at or below `box_low + 1*ATR`, plan one BUY limit exactly at the box low for the *next* four-hour bar. If the current close lies at or above `box_high - 1*ATR` and strictly below `box_high - 0.25*ATR`, plan one SELL limit exactly at the box high. Otherwise emit no H10 plan; if both sides somehow qualify, emit none. Native stop is 0.25 prior ATR **outside** that edge, limit target is the arithmetic box midpoint, and a remaining position time-exits after 30 completed four-hour bars. Require valid positive Instrument-rounded entry/stop/target ordering, one live entry or position per coin, and never place an order after observing the touch. The edge width, buffer and 60 bars reuse H03's frozen scale; the one-ATR approach band, midpoint target and one-bar expiry are declared researcher assumptions now, not selected from the viewed year's PnL. Use H01's frozen native equity-risk size of 0.25% per planned stop with a 5% per-coin notional cap, including Instrument minimum/precision skips. No strength, trend-line, Fibonacci, extra coin, target sweep or post-fill stop movement is admitted.

**Gates and economic comparison:** Synthetic causal cases must distinguish lower/upper edge, unarmed range middle, box failure, current-bar hindsight and one-bar native expiry. A BTC/ETH pilot checks filled OTO protection, targets/stops, denial/rejection and source-date category limits; its PnL cannot select the 37-coin run. If integrity passes, replay **all 37** on the identical registered five-minute LAST/MARK/funding and current-Instrument Catalogs, 100,000-USDT shared margin account, 1x leverage, order eligibility 2025-10-17 00:00 UTC, end 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC, and the native fee/funding model. Run a **same-window, same-risk-budget R-1u** account as paired control rather than comparing H10 to H01's earlier October 7 eligibility. Preserve exact source/input hashes, all native reports, open protection, closed-position win rate/payoff, final equity and 365-day annualized return/Sharpe, daily-close drawdown, minute-drawdown availability, fees/funding and per-coin counts. Report a conditional source proxy separately from verified execution and economics. Because this year and the box definition are already exposed, even a favorable complete result remains multiple-tested development evidence; no H10 parameter is revised on this year and independent delayed/forward qualification remains required. A failed source or order-integrity gate is retained with its cause rather than silently turned into an economic score.

## Diagnostic D35: can a fixed +1R partial exit alone rescue H10's win-rate gap?

**Registered before the excursion read:** H10's 61 wins among 934 closed native positions require at least **453 additional positive positions** to reach the already registered 55% lower win screen on the *same trade set*. S02 and H04 establish that staged exits are a relevant source/product path, but no source claims H10's exact +1R split. Reuse the frozen D05/D06 `analyze_exits.py` on H10's actual native entry and linked stop orders and the registered five-minute LAST Catalog; its already fixed 0.5R/1R/1.5R/2R thresholds are diagnostic only. Count stopped positions whose favorable high/low crossed +1R in a full intervening bar, and the permissive count including ambiguous entry/exit bars. The decision statistic is `(61 + permissive stopped positions reaching +1R) / 934`. If it remains below 55%, do **not** implement an H10-only +1R partial-exit economic sweep as a Goal candidate; prioritize a distinct source-context and structural-invalidation entry mechanism. If it reaches 55%, a full native partial-exit order lifecycle could be considered only after separate preregistration and source-case checks. Neither count is a realized win, fee-inclusive return, alternate fill path or independent holdout. Preserve source/input hashes, same-bar ambiguity and every result; do not select a different threshold after this read.

**Read and diagnostic repair:** The first attempt stopped before output because the D05/D06 script required exactly two native fill events per position; H10 has 11 closed multi-event positions from partial entry/exit fills (seven non-wins). `analyze_exits.py` was extended with an explicit opt-in that leaves their excursions unresolved and generously treats all seven non-wins as rescued in the upper bound; default D05 behavior remains unchanged. A regression read of the D05 run reproduced its 1,625 closed positions, 595 winners and 257 permissive +1R stopped paths, with zero unresolved complex paths. The repaired script SHA-256 is `aac45002f2aa722cbe8a4338d6cd68aed899df066b3a421c2b767a175f771693`; its H10 report `results/2026-10-08-h10-exit-opportunity-d35.json` SHA-256 `5b2eb98bd6fb016d2a147e292068052f3fd1cb7c031b4750d25c5a787181d383` reproduced byte-for-byte on a second run and binds native positions SHA-256 `d088e962dc506026a2f709e5f6a4f53c2257bea634a4c4edccbee518e7d74d87` and orders SHA-256 `f0ae85dd4f13ea2f6d198f00f8a14dd7e162d3b76df154e733741b` to the reused LAST Catalog.

**Observed opportunity and decision:** Of 874 native stop closures, **338** reached +1R in a full interior five-minute bar and **537** only under the permissive boundary-inclusive read. The strict same-set optimistic win rate is `(61+338)/934 = 42.72%`; the permissive rate, also treating seven unresolved complex non-wins as rescued, is `(61+537+7)/934 = 64.78%`. **305** simple positions have no full interior bar. Of the 199 permissive-only +1R paths, 97 are same-event entry/stop bars and 102 occur at another boundary bar; the OHLC high/low may precede entry or follow exit. The registered permissive screen passes, so the +1R staged branch remains testable, but the strict evidence alone cannot close the 55% win gap. A native order-lifecycle replay is needed to learn which boundary opportunities can actually fill after the entry; the MFE counts are not executable wins or net equity. Proceed with one frozen native partial-exit child rather than selecting another target from this year's 0.5R/1.5R/2R diagnostic columns. Source-context and structural-stop work remains a separate child, not ruled out by D35.
