## Candidate H12: R-1u trend entry after an outer four-hour range break

**Registered before H12 implementation or economic read:** This tests a different entry-selection mechanism from H11, not a rescue of its staged exit. S13 SOL describes a narrow triangle-line break *inside* a wider box as insufficient for a new breakout entry; S13 LINK warns that breaking one line need not reverse higher-high/low structure. S11/S12 MATIC allows a short-term line-break long *toward* its prior high, so the outer-break condition is scoped only to R-1u's **new daily trend-following 2R position**, not declared a universal Ronnie rule. D22 found 544/630 H06 four-hour line-break signals inside the prior 60-bar outer edge and 340 with the edge before their 2R target, demonstrating a distinct context category without saying which category wins. The exact 60-bar high/low proxy and its use with R-1u are researcher choices, not the author's chart anchors or private orders. The frozen 60-bar scale already exists in H03/D22; it is not chosen from H01/H11 PnL.

**One causal filter:** Retain R-1u's confirmed daily pivots, strong candle/trend test, resting role-reversal limit, 10-day expiry, original 0.25 daily ATR structural stop, fixed 2R native target and 60-day time exit. At each *completed* daily signal with availability boundary `T`, form the high/low of exactly **60 contiguous completed four-hour LAST bars ending no later than `T - 1 day`**. This fixes the outer range before the signal day begins; the daily signal candle itself cannot redefine the edge. Admit a long only when that daily close is strictly above the prior high, or a short only when strictly below the prior low. Equality, incomplete four-hour coverage or fewer than 60 bars rejects the signal and is counted distinctly. Apply before the existing waiting/order path, leaving one live entry/position per coin and all Nautilus execution, fee, MARK/funding, account and risk mechanisms unchanged. Use H01's frozen native 25-bp equity-at-stop risk and 5% per-coin notional cap; no alternate box width, ATR clearance, stop/target, side, coin or parameter sweep is admitted. The filter asks whether rejecting smaller/internal breaks can move **both** win rate from H01's 36.62% toward 55-65% and annual return beyond 20% without the H01 drawdown/Sharpe tradeoff. On H01's fixed 1,625-position set, even a hypothetical perfect winner-preserving filter needs to reject at least 634 of 1,030 losses for 60%; H12 can also change slots and later entries, so this is a scale test rather than a predicted H12 trade count.

**Falsifiers, gates and custody:** A synthetic causal check must show a close outside a frozen prior box admits its daily break, while a close inside, equality, incomplete 60-bar history and a break arising only from the current day's high do not; four-hour and daily timestamp ordering must not change the result. A BTC/ETH native pilot checks subscribed bars, accepted OTO stop/target, expiry, terminal open-position protection and denials/rejections. Its PnL does not choose whether to run all 37; if integrity passes, run the full reused 2025-10-07 to 2026-10-07 LAST/MARK/funding Catalogs, one 100,000-USDT account, order eligibility 2025-10-17, and an exact-source paired unfiltered R-1u control with the same 25-bp/5% sizing. Retain all attempts, source and input hashes, signal filtering counts, native orders/fills/positions/equity, net annualized return, closed-position win rate/payoff, Sharpe(365), daily-close drawdown, commissions/funding, paired weekly uncertainty and limitations. If the source category cannot be implemented causally or the native order gate fails, repair or retain the failed attempt before economic comparison; do not choose a favorable cutoff after seeing PnL. Any positive annual result is another exposed-year development test, subject to the complete attempt census and later delayed/forward qualification, not immediate Goal completion. A negative result closes only this 60-bar outer-break filter for daily 2R entries, leaving source-supported support overlap and structural invalidation as distinct children.

**One frozen causal rule:** On every completed four-hour LAST bar, use only contiguous history and the previous completed bar's native Wilder ATR(14). If absent, emit no setup. Require B no older than **12** completed four-hour bars (H03's already frozen retest horizon). Set 50%, 61.8%, 76.4% to `B - ratio*(B-A)`. ## Diagnostic D38: joint-goal capacity of a stop-avoidance child

**Question and status:** This is a post hoc arithmetic screen on the already exposed H13f aggregate and D37 native exit attribution, not a pre-registered strategy, alternate fill model, or new economic run. Before coding another stop filter, ask whether avoiding the observed losing stops *alone*, while leaving every other recorded trade and account component fixed, could meet both Goal floors. Inputs are H13f `results/2026-10-08-h13f-37-summary.json` SHA-256 `a401a4edd0d0412726b59adf1d96769a0fe6af26c5bdef9711efb78951994f5e` and D37 `results/2026-10-08-h13f-exit-attribution-d37.json` SHA-256 `0ca0903fb582d93466b3acce7953a14e0173b78452ff5b26f1582cc5994b25de`. The account starts at 100,000 USDT and ends at 100,273.62941887; the 2025-10-17 00:00 to 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC reporting window is 0.97357305936 years. There are 64 wins among 177 closed native positions, including 104 losing stop closures totaling -16,906.56872472 USDT.

**Fixed-outcome arithmetic:** Keeping all 64 winners and removing only losers requires removing at least **61 of 113 losses** for a 55% win rate, or **71** for 60%; if only stop closures can be removed, these are respectively **61/104 = 58.65%** and **71/104 = 68.27%** of the observed stops. A strictly greater than 20% annualized return over the exact reporting window requires final equity strictly above **119,423.20655419 USDT**, an increase exceeding **19,149.57713532 USDT** from H13f. Even giving back the *entire* observed stop-loss PnL while leaving all other account components intact yields only **117,180.19814359 USDT**, or **17.69% annualized**. Thus a pure deletion of this fixed set of stop losses cannot by itself meet the return floor, even though such a hypothetical deletion would easily raise the win fraction.

**Decision and limits:** These are necessary-scale calculations for one frozen trade/account path, not a bound on a re-run Strategy: skipped entries alter slot availability, later signals, risk sizing, fees, funding and open equity. A source-grounded H13g or another location child may still be worth testing, but its proposal must say what additional mechanism could create enough *new* winning opportunity, larger payoff, or effective capital use to cross the return gap while retaining a near-60% win rate. If it only promises fewer known stop trades, keep it as a narrow diagnostic child rather than presenting it as a plausible complete Goal candidate. Rank the next experiment by a causal source-case falsifier and its plausible joint-goal effect; do not choose filters or thresholds by the exposed losing positions. Preserve H13f as the exact native paired control. This screen changes no Strategy, historical result, or Goal status.

**Mechanical incompatibility:** With impulse span `D = B - A > 0`, a 50% long entry is `B - 0.5D`, a stop *below* the 76.4% level is `< B - 0.764D`, and target `B` gives reward `0.5D` and risk `> 0.264D`; therefore room is **strictly below `0.5 / 0.264 = 1.89394R`**, even before native price rounding. Reusing H13c's January 6 BTC source-matching A=87,189.2, B=94,760.3 and frozen stop=88,752.99164159 gives a 50% entry at 90,974.75 and **1.70385R** to B. Its universal `>=2R` room gate would necessarily reject this S24 upper-tier/stop/target combination. This is a scope mismatch between one frozen researcher rule and another author-discussed *conditional style*, not a bug in Nautilus, a proof that the author entered at 50%, or a license to lower the existing H13c/H13f gate after viewing annual PnL.

**Decision:** Preserve H13c/H13f exactly. A distinct prospective 50% entry style could add opportunities rather than merely filter H13f stops, making it relevant to D38's joint-goal gap, but S36 did not verify the prior BTC 50% plan and S24's 50% was already under test when discussed. Before any economic H14, obtain a genuinely pre-touch source case or explicitly register a researcher proxy with independent positive/negative source predictions, causal A/B and first-visible timing, a style-specific stop/obstacle and risk budget. Then test native execution and the full 37-coin shared account with exact paired control; one exposed-year score cannot qualify the Goal. Do not silently substitute SUI tiers or PEPE invalidation into BTC, and do not infer that a sub-2R trade is profitable from a chart annotation.

## Diagnostic D44: can a +1R-only exit rescue H14a's fixed trade-set win-rate gap?

**Registered before price-excursion read:** H14a's 254 wins among 651 native closed positions need at least **105** additional positive outcomes to reach 55%, or **137** to reach 60%, assuming all 651 trades remain and no current win is lost. D43 shows 330 native stop closures with zero wins. If even the permissive ceiling plus existing wins is below 359/651 (55%), close the +1R-only child before native implementation. If the strict count is at least 137, one separately preregistered native partial-exit test may be justified; an intermediate result requires event-order and return-capacity analysis before choosing a child. This fixed-trade-set opportunity screen cannot prove an executable partial fill, a higher portfolio return or a 60% qualified strategy; a partial exit could shrink payoff further. Do not choose a different R level or exit fraction using the exposed H14a PnL.

**D44 read and decision:** `results/2026-10-08-h14a-exit-opportunity-d44.json` SHA-256 `916b23d4922fe69bbe694dc8d2f9f5b0db2fa8e01b3ac126b4b59f491aad1eb2` matches the frozen H14a native order and position hashes and reconciles **651** closed / **254** positive positions. ## Diagnostic D45: source-stop and tier-room geometry before another native variant

**Registered comparison on already exposed sources, before the new arithmetic read:** H14a uses a standalone 50% limit and `76.4%-0.25*prior_ATR` stop; S27 BTC discusses three separately budgeted tiers with one valid stop below a rising line **or** prior low, while S24 BTC says at least below 76.4%. Bind S27 source SHA-256 `284b15c1f4ac5476e8d4f548ffa46891c40a163965fe69b4c09e07fcdfe15d1c`, S24 source `4cac7ad01f5825513f615dad9d73abc946c43bbba93dd1417fb22c7e1bdfd577`, H14a source gate `99a7280b6fd5f187fc626dedf1c96092bfb18da206f0b9b7c213c5c9630f8084`, and D41 SUI candidate-set `490a93b32fed4d816463d04774ed610f96327ffbaff948ef57c228ecc96e108f`. For each frozen S27 00:00/04:00 and S24 00:00/04:00 cutoff, report A, B, 50/61.8/76.4 tiers, H14a stop, the stop's distance above/below A, target-to-stop R at 50%, and whether an untouched live plan existed. Separately calculate the universal *ceiling* of target-at-B R when a common stop is at or below A for each tier: at stop=A the ceiling is `r/(1-r)` for retracement fraction `r`, and below-A is strictly smaller. Do not digitize an approximate rising line from video pixels, equate A to that line, or call a stop above A invalid under the source's line alternative. If the standalone 1.5R-at-50% floor conflicts with the below-A stop path, treat a common-stop/multiple-tier approach as a **distinct** hypothesis requiring a separately fixed anchor, line/low invalidation, budget, native simultaneous-order protection and capacity gate; do not silently widen H14a's stop or reuse its annual score. Preserve S24's post-touch timing and D41's four SUI anchor matches as unresolved scope, not rescued positives. No fills, PnL or parameter sweep follow from D45.

**D45 read and consequence:** The source-only `audit_h14a_stop_geometry.py` SHA-256 `b9970bd23cd3917bc314e459129c890c1f85b689a749cd1262e7a9cc430c39d5` produced `results/2026-10-08-d45-source-stop-geometry.json` SHA-256 `fbd1fa189f62014569debbb0efb69f4fd3a4ebe778de92b48f3c4317837a2482`. At S27 00:00, H14a's stop **68,655.90** is **944.90 USDT above** its selected A=67,711, with target at B yielding **1.5801R**; the 04:00 recalculated stop remains about **948.54 above A**. At S24 00:00, the stop **88,704.72** is **1,515.52 above A=87,189.2**, while the 50% tier had already been touched; at 04:00 H14a has no selected recent impulse. Algebraically, if a different common stop is at the selected A, target B provides only **1R** from 50%, **1.6178R** from 61.8% and **3.2373R** from 76.4%; a stop *below* A provides less. Thus H14a's 50%-tier **>=1.5R** condition and a below-A common-stop branch cannot both use B as target. This does **not** show H14a violated S27's *line* stop alternative: no causal line price was measured. It shows that a below-A structural-stop, budgeted three-tier system is a separate rule with different payoff and native order state, not a safe stop-parameter edit to H14a. D41's four SUI fitting origins per cutoff still prevent author-exact cross-asset anchor claims. No economic estimate or revised H14a score follows.

## Candidate H15a: budgeted three-tier pullback with one below-origin stop

**Registered before implementation, source gate and annual economics:** H14a's standalone 50% plan has 651 closed positions but only 39.02% wins, 1.32 realized payoff and -10.4350% annualized; D44 rules out a +1R-only rescue of its unchanged trade set. S27 explicitly describes optional 50%/61.8%/76.4% entries funded across tiers with a common valid stop below the rising line **or** previous low, while D45 shows that the below-A branch is materially different from H14a's stop. H15a tests the *below selected A* branch as a named researcher proxy. The source does not specify exact allocation fractions or a unique cross-asset A selector; retain those limits instead of claiming author-exactness. This is a potential new opportunity/payoff structure, not a stop tweak or addition of separate H13/H14 PnLs.

**Frozen rule and capacity check:** Reuse H14a's already registered four-hour LAST/ATR A/B selector, pair supersession, 180-bar lookback, latest order-8 confirmed low, earliest prior-60 maximum high, B age <=12, six-bar span, first untouched 50% decision and one live bundle per coin. At a valid completed decision bar, calculate three Instrument-rounded BUY limits at `B-r*(B-A)` for `r=0.5, 0.618, 0.764`; use one fixed common stop `A-0.25*prior_ATR` and first/only target B. Require all three prices and positive stop distance after Instrument rounding; **do not** apply H14a's incompatible 1.5R-to-B per-tier floor. At bundle creation freeze native-equity-based quantity for each tier as Instrument-rounded `min((equity*0.0025/3)/(entry-stop), (equity*0.05/3)/entry)`. Thus the three possible fills share a total **25-bp stop-risk ceiling** and **5% aggregate coin notional cap** at the same account snapshot, with 1x shared margin; unused tier budget is not reassigned. Admit a bundle only if all three native quantities pass exchange minima, and do not change quantities when a later tier fills. Each limit expires after 30 completed four-hour bars; after the first fill, close all filled exposure after 30 such bars if neither common stop nor B target has closed it, and cancel every remaining pending tier on bundle closure. A changed selected A/B before any fill cancels all three limits and waits for native cancel acknowledgment before a replacement. If an old entry fills before those acknowledgments, keep its native protection and cancel the unfinished replacement; already requested sibling cancellations may finish, so do not falsely report a full three-tier fill. Otherwise, after any fill retain the frozen old bundle and any still-active tiers until its closure. The risk ceiling is over all three filled tiers, not 25 bp *per* tier; the max theoretical full-bundle return at B is bounded by the three D45 R ceilings and shrinks with a below-A ATR buffer, fees and cap. This provides *possible* capacity but no forecast or proof of >20% annualized.

**Source, execution and economic gates:** At frozen S27 BTC 2026-04-09 00:00/04:00 UTC, require the selected A/B in the original source bands, all three tiers below the decision close and untouched since B, one proposed bundle with the common stop below A and B as first obstacle. S24 2026-01-08 00:00/04:00 remains a **post-50%-touch sensitivity**, never a newly armed three-tier video order; C19 ETH 2026-04-13 00:00/04:00 remains a wrong-anchor negative; D41/S36 SUI remains a named four-hour A-selector mismatch at all four cutoffs. Failure to form S27 or a false source claim stops this child before annual economics. A pass admits only this scoped researcher proxy. Use Nautilus native entry/exit orders and Portfolio/account state; no parallel fill engine or ledger. Before a real-data pilot, synthetic native event cases must cover three pending limits, one/two/three staggered fills, common-stop or target closure, same-event bar ambiguity, new-pair cancellation acknowledgment, fill-before-cancel, time exit, no residual reduce-only exits and no excess net short. BTC/ETH native pilot then checks order/fill/protection identity and zero denied/rejected orders; pilot PnL cannot select the full replay. If integrity passes, replay all 37 exact existing LAST/MARK/funding Catalogs in one 100,000-USDT account, same eligible window, fees/funding and quantities, plus an exact-input/risk/window H14a control under the same runner regardless of pilot return. Preserve all attempts, source hashes, native raw-report hashes, win/payoff, annualized net equity, Sharpe(365), daily and available minute drawdown, protected opens and paired weekly uncertainty. The year is multiply exposed development data; a successful point estimate still requires delayed/forward and multiplicity-aware qualification before the Goal can complete.

## Diagnostic D49: whether an unfilled tier bundle survives a revisit of its first target

**Registered before this diagnostic reader or result:** D48 found more H16a stops with fewer trades but could not attribute individual paths. S27 names the prior high B as the first obstacle for the pullback plan; S29 says the high is updated after a new effective high. A causal, researcher-defined bundle-retirement question is whether a native plan can remain open after LAST price has already revisited its B target **while none of its tiers has filled**, then fill on a later pullback. This is not an author-stated cancellation command or a new backtest. Bind the exact existing paired H15a/H16a native `orders.csv`, `fills.csv`, `positions.csv` and summaries plus the frozen 37-coin LAST Catalog/input identity. Reconstruct each submitted bundle from its actual native OTO parent and child order IDs, same Instrument, submission time, common stop and B target; reject ambiguous grouping or any parent/child/fill mismatch. For each bundle with a later first native entry fill, inspect only complete five-minute LAST bars **strictly after submission and strictly before the first-fill bar**. Count a pre-fill B revisit only if a bar high reaches the native rounded B target and its low remains above the bundle's highest entry price; this excludes same-bar sequencing and any bar that could itself have filled a tier. Do not use MARK for entry/target touch. Report by H15a and H16a: submitted bundles, first fills, pre-fill B revisits, later filled entries, closed positions and their native closing reason/PnL, and missing/ambiguous paths. Reconcile native fill/position totals to the frozen reports; no per-tier synthetic PnL or cross-strategy order-level counterfactual.

**Decision rule and limits:** If this path is absent or rare, do not prioritize a B-revisit cancellation child as the next Goal-return mechanism. On H15a's fixed 676 closed positions, deleting losses while preserving 382 wins would require at least 40 losses for 60% wins; separately, moving its exact reported final equity above the >20% annualized threshold requires more than **15,262.61 USDT**. Compare the count and absolute loss of *actual* post-revisit losing positions with these two necessary scales. Even exceeding both is only a descriptive capacity screen: cancellation changes later slots, sizing, fees, funding and path, so no old-trade deletion is a tradable return or win-rate forecast. That does not identify whether an initially promising entry later loses its premise; shortening all holding times could remove slow winners. **Frozen observation and reconciliation:** For every native H15a position, replay its actual `OrderFilled` and `PositionAdjusted` events through Nautilus `Position`, require exact final realized-PnL reconciliation for each closed position and count all ten still open separately. Take the first actual opening fill and its native OTO stop as the fixed first-exposure R; later entry fills stay in the same native netting position and are flagged, never given fabricated tier PnL. Observe at the closes of the **first, third, fifth and tenth fully completed four-hour LAST bars after the first fill**; the entry-containing bar is excluded so its high/low cannot leak pre-fill movement. A landmark is observed only while the actual native position is still open strictly after that bar's close. Closing before or at a landmark is censored, grouped by actual native stop/target/market closing order; an open position at run end is right-censored. At each observable landmark report elapsed time, high/low MFE/MAE since the first eligible bar in first-exposure R, close displacement in R, native Position PnL marked to the LAST close, the indicative fee-inclusive immediate-exit value using the instrument's taker fee, and eventual realized PnL of the actual native continuation. The indicative exit is an arithmetic screen only: it is neither a tradable fill nor a new account/ledger. Report whether a later tier enters after the landmark and stratify the comparison; future path associations must never be presented as causal gains.

**Frozen question and decision boundary:** The primary screen is at **three** complete post-entry four-hour bars (roughly 12-16 hours after a fill): `MFE < +0.5 first-exposure R` means the planned move has not advanced even halfway to its first risk unit. The `+0.5R` reference is an old diagnostic convention, not fitted on D53. One/five/ten bars and nonpositive close displacement are descriptive sensitivities, not selectable thresholds. Count slow survivors, their eventual winners including target exits, censored early stop/target/time paths, net native continuation minus indicative immediate exit and the first-entry versus added-tier strata. If there are fewer than **30** slow survivors, or they include substantial later winners/target gains, this exact blanket three-bar exit lead is rejected; otherwise a positive arithmetic screen can only admit a separate source/structure rationale and **one frozen native Strategy child** with complete 37-coin shared-account replay and exact paired H15a control. No annual return is obtained by summing the indicative exits, and no rule is selected from the viewed year's best landmark or coin. ## Candidate H19a / source-capacity diagnostic D60: a distinct broad-swing two-tier pullback

Among order-8 pivot lows already confirmed **by B's completion**, at least six bars before B and within the preceding **180** complete four-hour bars, choose the **lowest price**, breaking a tie toward the earlier pivot, as A. ## Diagnostic D62: can an already registered first-risk-unit target possibly repair H19a's win-rate gap?

**Registered before the H19a excursion read:** H19a has 212 positive native closed positions among 496, so even on its fixed trade set a 60% screen needs at least **298** winners, or **86** additional positions converted without losing a winner; the older 55% lower screen needs **273**, or **61** more. They do not prove that Ronnie uses a full +1R exit for H19a or that a staged target will preserve return. Bind the accepted H19a summary SHA-256 `7d95658949badb9b76ead4b7fcbebda8b5901c836b332bc9bec57dc0d859f7ec`, native orders `9bc7228673948e0299e2f208405d870eb7c8b2215ce44b0b10006102da97feaa`, fills `3b798730719e633f02bcd2fc05977cacbeaecb539d9de584536bc25a71ffa391`, positions `54e211bcf50de11269800a2c7f8e0e2a77e3704a1efd30292e60b7ec96a77a3a`, passing independent audit `f9297dc270bae5b692d3066adc7cb1eda1b218b69fe030b6f4434f5816bb882a`, and the registered 37 native five-minute LAST Catalog tree identities. Do not reuse the older single-fill `analyze_exits.py` on H19a's multi-entry positions.

**Fixed method and decision:** For every **closed** native H19a position, take its first actual BUY fill and that entry's linked original native OTO stop as the first-exposure price risk R, while retaining all later tier fills, partial fills, position funding and final native PnL as facts. Reconcile all 496 closed positions, 212 winners and 11 right-censored open positions; no position is dropped for complex native fills. Read each coin's complete five-minute LAST highs strictly **after** the first fill event and strictly **before** the final native closing fill event to count nonwinning positions that reached at least **+1 first-exposure R** before ending nonpositive. Also report a deliberately permissive count including entry/exit boundary bars, same-event closures, closing-order type and later-tier count; the boundary count can include movement before entry or after exit and is only an upper opportunity count. Do not infer an executable fill from a bar high or allocate fake partial-tier PnL. If even the permissive count is below **86**, reject this exact all-at-1R or guaranteed-win-rescue mechanism as a path to approximately 60% on the fixed H19a trades; if strict count is at least **86**, it only admits a separately preregistered source/OTO lifecycle candidate and a full 37-coin same-account paired native test. Intermediate counts call for event-order inspection before any child. In every case separately report current winners' native target exits and original target-B distance in first-exposure R so their sacrificed payoff remains visible; an opportunity count does not establish >20% annual return, unchanged slots, or independent edge. No scan of alternate R thresholds, time windows, coins or staged fractions on this exposed year.

**D62 complete read and decision:** The frozen reader SHA-256 `d547592f4e871cd7e4eb70a17600f73a987a7bfc6c41a694ccfc96250bbc0857` reconciled all **496** closed native positions, **212** positive closes, and **11** right-censored open positions. The optimistic fixed-set win rates would be **54.84%** (strict) and **56.45%** (permissive), assuming every reached nonwinner became a winner without losing an existing winner or changing the trade set; these are bounds on that simple rescue story, **not** executable backtests. Otherwise inspect the specific binding path before proposing an intervention. ## Diagnostic D64: which current sizing bound actually controls the native entry tiers?

**Research input and boundary, before geometry read:** The user proposes examining Kelly sizing and its variants because position management is immature. [Kelly's original paper](https://onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/10.1002/j.1538-7305.1956.tb03809.x) is about long-run logarithmic capital growth under known opportunity probabilities and odds; [MacLean, Thorp and Ziemba](https://escholarship.org/uc/item/5mr5k8qj) explain full Kelly's large short-horizon losses and fractional Kelly's growth/security tradeoff; [Busseti, Ryu and Boyd](https://www.web.stanford.edu/~boyd/papers/kelly.html) add an explicit drawdown-risk constraint. These are method sources, **not** evidence that Ronnie uses Kelly or that the H19a/H18a one-year outcome distribution is known. Source case C20 supports splitting a fixed budget across prospective tiers with one structural stop, but gives no numeric fraction. H19a and H18a currently size each native tier as the minimum of **25-bp total Portfolio-equity stop risk** divided equally by tier count and **5% per-coin notional** divided equally; 37 independent Strategies share one Nautilus account, with no aggregate portfolio-risk target or uncertainty estimate. The observed year is development-exposed, and its variable multi-tier, partly filled, time-exited, fee/funding-adjusted positions and correlated overlapping coin exposures do not satisfy a two-outcome, independent, known-odds Kelly plug-in. No Kelly fraction will be estimated or selected from the annual scores in D64.

**Preregistered read-only test:** Bind H19a `summary.json` SHA-256 `7d95658949badb9b76ead4b7fcbebda8b5901c836b332bc9bec57dc0d859f7ec` and `orders.csv` `9bc7228673948e0299e2f208405d870eb7c8b2215ce44b0b10006102da97feaa`; pair H18a `summary.json` `6298b850bc3100c7845dc9ee801ae4a899f3a94c64a5004ec2bdf0c61a8b8c80` and `orders.csv` `35169fc9298c78e6b15e73b958fa147bfb018485571bc3122ce7cbc36410d763`. For every native BUY LIMIT entry, join its unique original parent-linked native STOP_MARKET and require positive `entry - stop`. The algebraic risk-versus-notional branch is determined by `d=(entry-stop)/entry` against the frozen `25 bp / 5% = 5%` threshold: risk cap controls if `d > 5%`, notional cap if `d < 5%`, tie otherwise. Report exact tier counts, distribution of `d`, per-coin coverage and any missing/ambiguous protection; do not read PnL per tier, rescale fills, sweep budget fractions or compute counterfactual account returns. If risk cap controls a majority of H19a tiers, increasing the notional cap alone cannot enlarge most unchanged submitted quantities; if notional cap controls a majority, increasing the stop-risk budget alone cannot enlarge most. Either is a **mechanical diagnosis only**, not a reason to raise that cap. A Kelly-family native candidate first needs a causal, net-of-cost edge distribution, estimation uncertainty, overlapping-risk/correlation and drawdown constraints, plus the same shared-account and delayed/forward validation rules. No current-year optimum will be selected.

**D64 complete read and decision:** Frozen native reader `5c4c44a03`, SHA-256 `a8a3e61ed81ee14bdcee3b3e729128e70f7471225b582add680fc25aea769c91`, produced [the two-run geometry audit](results/2026-10-08-d64-sizing-geometry.json), SHA-256 `72128968925108f446ad64c2f8f1ae3456906fd6de179232b93481e3a6e1c6c5`. Every native entry has exactly one original parent-linked STOP_MARKET: **5,878/5,878** H19a and **9,813/9,813** H18a, across all 37 coins. H19a has **3,138 risk-bound tiers (53.39%)**, **2,739 notional-bound (46.60%)**, and one raw tie; initial stop-distance median is **5.28%**, 90th percentile **11.79%**. H18a has **6,381 risk-bound tiers (65.03%)** and **3,432 notional-bound (34.97%)**, with median stop distance **6.38%** and 90th percentile **14.09%**. The majority-risk gate holds for H19a, so merely increasing the 5% per-coin notional cap leaves most *unchanged raw* entry sizes controlled by 25-bp stop risk. Conversely, increasing only 25 bp leaves H19a's 2,739 notional-bound tiers unchanged at the raw formula. Instrument quantity rounding may obscure which cap produced the same final integer lot. This audit creates no new orders, return, win rate or Sharpe, and it does not license increasing either cap. C20 establishes tier capital allocation but gives no numeric total risk budget; D64's preregistration phrase "fixed budget" names the existing implementation, not a proven Ronnie-specified number. The next research task is a **causal estimator-feasibility and shared-risk audit** before freezing one fractional/drawdown-constrained Kelly-family candidate: use actual native net-of-cost position and account paths, distinguish trade outcome from initial stop risk, count overlapping correlated exposures, quantify time-ordered estimation uncertainty and predeclare bounds. The exposed year can develop and falsify the method but cannot independently qualify it.

## Diagnostic D65: is the native portfolio log-growth estimate stable enough to inform Kelly sizing?

**Registered before this log-growth read:** Keep H19a and unchanged H18a in the same 100,000-USDT shared-account configuration. Bind H19a `summary.json` SHA-256 `7d95658949badb9b76ead4b7fcbebda8b5901c836b332bc9bec57dc0d859f7ec` and native `returns_series.csv` `02297c7a5183f64d7ad4d65b19e2bc9bc06b871d4dd6da3f4a444e28f7b4729c`; H18a equivalents `6298b850bc3100c7845dc9ee801ae4a899f3a94c64a5004ec2bdf0c61a8b8c80` and `25a643ac845e9a527528adc9221acd6c0a9919d7587d51957b78e21bdc4f8c1a`. The already accepted native D61 capital reports are H19a `91a1cfc6b944685bd4e8d933b995df951460368f86f6d7cb9834869195f6889f` and H18a `d309d3b04fc440c107849c60fcc2f002aa19221d79bb83b9a63e9777d034a352`; use their simultaneous-open-position counts as descriptive concurrency evidence, without treating every coin as an independent bet. The native account results include fees and funding; the series is daily Portfolio returns, not independently realized trade R multiples.

**Fixed read and gate:** Restrict both daily native return series to their shared first order-eligible time **2025-10-17 00:00 UTC** through final returned daily sample no later than **2026-10-07 08:30 UTC**. Require identical increasing timestamps, finite returns greater than -100%, and no gaps in the UTC daily grid. For each run compute `mean(log1p(native_return))`, its `exp(365 * mean_log)-1` annualized equivalent, and an exploratory **5,000-draw ISO-week block bootstrap** (seed **20261008**) of that annualized equivalent, resampling entire observed week blocks with replacement, preserving each block's within-week days. Report the 2.5/97.5 percentiles and whether the interval includes zero. Also at each completed ISO-week boundary after **13 complete weeks** calculate the sign of the mean log return using only prior eligible daily samples; report positive/negative/zero sign counts and first/last sign, without selecting a lookback. Reconcile the eligible day count with the existing 356-day paired read and state any partial-final-day gap to the native final equity. If the block interval includes zero, the exposed native series does not establish a stable positive **current-size log-growth estimate** for setting a Kelly fraction; even an entirely positive interval would remain an exposed, unadjusted development result, not an independent edge. A zero-crossing interval does not disprove that a smaller, differently managed strategy might have positive log growth. No `f` optimization, scaling of historical returns into orders, retroactive sizing, or new backtest economics in D65.

**D65 result and next mechanism:** Frozen reader `3ed4aa510`, SHA-256 `ba621afb2bba625f21349a0a08b184945aa16c926bfb197643d73db1588a4c15`, produced [the paired native-return diagnostic](results/2026-10-08-d65-kelly-estimator.json), SHA-256 `6a083b243631027b11465bbb685b199ccd1619d67d39df987d943ab70fc81c2b`. Both runs have the same **356** contiguous eligible daily samples and **52** ISO-week blocks; the last daily sample precedes final Portfolio equity by **8.5 hours**. At unchanged sizing, H19a's observed annualized log-growth equivalent is **+11.98%**, close to but distinct from its final-equity **+12.00%** annualization; the unadjusted week-bootstrap interval is **[-26.61%, +73.21%]**. H18a's observed equivalent is **+5.03%** against final-equity **+5.04%**, with interval **[-15.78%, +30.06%]**. Both intervals include zero. Among 38 completed-week decision boundaries after the initial 13 full weeks, H19a's past-only cumulative log-growth sign is positive **12** times and negative **26** (first negative, last positive); H18a is positive **26**, negative **12**. The D61 native-mark occupancy is mean **11.99**, peak **29** simultaneous H19a coin positions and mean **6.39**, peak **31** for H18a. This is substantial concurrent exposure, not 496 or 676 independent binary wagers. Thus the registered gate fails: neither development run has a stable positive **current-size portfolio log-growth estimate** on this block read, and neither supplies a credible numerical Kelly fraction. The intervals are exploratory, unadjusted, depend on the observed week mix, and do not rule out positive log growth under another sizing or signal rule. D65 does not change native orders, fees, funding or account outcomes. Continue this direction at a distinct child: normalize actual net position outcomes by their decision-time initial stop risk and inspect concurrent risk/causal estimator feasibility before any fractional- or drawdown-constrained Kelly Strategy is frozen; no annual fraction sweep on the exposed year.

## Diagnostic D66: do actual net position outcomes provide causal per-coin Kelly evidence?

**Registered before native outcome normalization:** Keep the accepted H19a and H18a 37-coin paired runs and their original source/Strategy choices. Bind H19a `orders.csv` `9bc7228673948e0299e2f208405d870eb7c8b2215ce44b0b10006102da97feaa`, `fills.csv` `3b798730719e633f02bcd2fc05977cacbeaecb539d9de584536bc25a71ffa391`, and `positions.csv` `54e211bcf50de11269800a2c7f8e0e2a77e3704a1efd30292e60b7ec96a77a3a`; H18a equivalents `35169fc9298c78e6b15e73b958fa147bfb018485571bc3122ce7cbc36410d763`, `b92effc8ccbfced1c81f388f8f4ce4cc28b68517079057cc8c985ca016cba64a`, and `6555dbd3315ae11108198fc04116fb1da43d58c3207dd45a3f753e4995b5436e`, plus passing independent native audits H19a `f9297dc270bae5b692d3066adc7cb1eda1b218b69fe030b6f4434f5816bb882a` and H18a `aa84512bc6565542d6aabe9e9501215bceb24a5579fad57a76b1c55fa25cf24b`. Do not join `fills.csv.position_id` directly to `positions.csv.position_id`: the former is a per-coin strategy base ID, while actual netting positions have separate UUID suffixes. Read each native Position's own ordered `OrderFilled` events and `opening_order_id`, as D62 did.

**Fixed read-only method:** For each native position, join each BUY fill event to its unique parent-linked original SELL STOP_MARKET and sum `actual BUY fill quantity x (actual fill price - original stop trigger) x native position multiplier` over all entry fills. Require positive risk and preserve every later tier and partial fill. This is **gross entered initial stop risk across fills**, not peak simultaneous risk or a pre-entry forecast of which tiers will fill. For each **closed** position divide native final `realized_pnl` (already including native commissions and funding adjustment) by this denominator; retain all winners, nonwinners and same-event exits, and right-censor open positions rather than invent final PnL. Reconcile closed/open/win counts and summed native realized PnL with accepted summaries and audits. Report R-multiple distribution descriptively, count one versus multiple distinct filled entry orders, and count native closed positions per coin. At each position's **first opening order `ts_init`** (the sizing/submission clock), count only same-coin positions with native `ts_closed` strictly earlier; report the distribution and the fraction with at least **30** prior closed same-coin outcomes as a modest sample-depth marker, not a sufficient statistical guarantee. Never use that position's own outcome or later closes in its prior count. If even this marker rarely holds, a per-coin plug-in Kelly fraction is particularly unsupported; if it often holds, dependence, uncertainty and independent evidence remain gates. Do not estimate or optimize a Kelly fraction, alter order sizes, or infer full-account returns from R multiples. Any missing stop, invalid fill risk, unreconciled native position or denominator ambiguity fails the read and must be investigated without dropping trades.

**D66 complete read and decision:** Frozen reader `df182ab30`, SHA-256 `71aa5183e2903f953fab34228936b4765c2c4722eaa5c25a7836673eeb1e480f`, produced [the per-position native net-R result](results/2026-10-08-d66-native-position-r.json.gz), gzip SHA-256 `780da1817851913f64f5682207976f6e37d2821f5b9bb612434ac3ad36bab305` (uncompressed JSON SHA-256 `dedb4b7f59885bd12c90c56319600d703f1e3735e5381c6e7a1b90609a2e2f48`). Every position's BUY fill event has positive risk against its unique original OTO stop, and all native position PnL sums reconcile to the accepted summaries: H19a **507** positions, **496** closed, **212** positive, **11** open, realized PnL **11,358.26805943 USDT**; H18a **686**, **676**, **380**, **10**, realized PnL **4,941.20432294 USDT**. H19a's closed-position net/gross-entered-risk R has mean **+0.297**, median **-0.965**, 10th/90th percentiles **-1.024/+2.452**; H18a's are mean **+0.141**, median **+0.144**, and **-1.015/+1.177**. H19a has **127** closed positions with one and **369** with two distinct filled entry orders; H18a has **208/178/290** with one/two/three. Thus the original position-level win/payoff and a single first-fill risk do not describe the actual multi-tier wager. At the **first order submission** of every resulting position, the median number of earlier closed same-coin positions is **6** for H19a and **9** for H18a; the maxima are **20** and **29**, so **0/496** and **0/676** have even the registered 30-prior-outcome sample marker. This closes only an unshrunk *per-coin historical plug-in Kelly estimator from this one year* as a credible next sizing rule. It does not close pooled, hierarchical, fractional or drawdown-constrained methods; those must explicitly handle source heterogeneity, correlated overlapping bets, prior uncertainty and portfolio caps, then be preregistered and tested natively with delayed/forward qualification. D66 is not a new annual return or proof of a Kelly benefit. For Goal progress, prioritize a causal signal/exit improvement alongside any later sizing experiment: scaling H19a's fixed 42.74% winning trade set cannot itself produce near-60% wins.

## Diagnostic D67: do old H19a resting opportunities explain the win-rate gap?

**Registered before the age/outcome join:** This is a distinct child of D63's pending-order capital diagnosis. C20-C22 distinguish a future pullback, an updated effective high, and a four-hour range-middle wait; they do not prescribe a numeric order lifetime. H19a presently gives its broad-swing two-tier bundle 180 complete four-hour bars (30 days), while the prior H15a family used 30 bars (five days). The **30-bar** cutoff is inherited from that previously registered lifetime, not selected from H19a outcomes and not attributed to Ronnie. This read uses the new published-Nautilus rc3 37-coin H19a replay at `/tmp/nautilus-minimal-cleanup-h19a-37/`: `summary.json` SHA-256 `d6fd06da5cb743acf568a0c80dfb6b3d45342c816977b5e5c77d0b1625a153cb`, `orders.csv` `81c5feddd9cb44f50f6c83df921bc945b84a089bcee622fb291a7cfb1a1b8e96`, and `positions.csv` `498551ad70c41701778f9b004aaa77ec2d602de72846acfd0fa3efe2cfbde218`. The corresponding [cleanup parity receipt](../../strategies/r1/parity-cleanup-h19a.json) SHA-256 `9b5e57720e779cc03241545bd85542a31f1a23a241c587c3ddbde0c261e4df64` passes against the frozen fork native report. Strategy source SHA-256 is `f43cd4603794cb68232168c23c0739427c979e1f90ad6f76460d2d21a06935fc`; the new worktree begins at merge commit `3a67ba39b11d0b4c08743c1694ef22de156e93b0`. Schema inspection before this registration exposed one AAVE order whose first fill was about two weeks after submission and an unrelated FIL Position row; no aggregate age/outcome result was read.

**Frozen read and decision:** For each *actual* native Position, take its first BUY `OrderFilled` event and require its order ID to equal `opening_order_id`. Recover the plan decision time as that original GTD entry's `expire_time_ns - 180 * four hours`, require `ts_init` at or after it and before its first fill, and classify a position as **late** if its first fill is at or after `plan time + 30 * four hours`. Do not join `fills.csv.position_id` to the unique Position ID. Reconcile all closed/winning counts and the sum of native realized PnL to the native summary; leave open positions right-censored. Report early/late counts, net PnL (already including native fee/funding), wins, mean net PnL, payoff, first-fill age quantiles, and the late winners as a negative case. The fixed-set early-only win rate is an optimistic algebraic screen, **not** a Strategy replay: cancellation also changes later tier fills, future slots, sizing and account paths. Admit exactly one prospective five-day expiry Strategy child only if there are at least 30 late closed positions, their native net PnL sum is negative, **and** the fixed-set early-only win rate exceeds H19a's 42.74% by at least five percentage points. Otherwise close this specific time-only expiry lead and investigate pre-entry structural updates rather than scanning another day cutoff. Even if admitted, the child must use the same 37-coin 100,000-USDT Nautilus account, native fees/funding and protected orders, plus full paired and delayed/forward qualification. This diagnostic cannot prove an annual return or independent edge.

**D67 completed read:** Preregistration `cca38e84e` preceded frozen reader `ad2cf7f36`, reader SHA-256 `074e416a68e48760ece3d13cf36c6614e96bacbb628b6bff236c7135838ff51a`. The [complete native Position age read](results/2026-10-08-d67-h19a-first-fill-age.json), SHA-256 `f5f77c8731adc022b56a31d991361edb6eae6fe2d8cf15e7eea397212385ab0d`, reconciles all **507** Positions, **496** closed, **212** winning and native realized PnL including the 11 still-open positions. First-fill age from the *plan* has median **13.69** complete four-hour bars (2.28 days), p90 **49.86** bars (8.31 days), and maximum **170.81** bars (28.47 days). At the frozen five-day split, **105** closed and four open Positions first fill late; the closed late group has **39/105 = 37.14%** winners and realized net PnL **-1,059.24 USDT**. Those 39 late winners contribute **+11,161.52 USDT**, a concrete counterexample to treating all old orders as failed signals. The early group's fixed set is **173/391 = 44.25%** winning, only **+1.50 percentage points** above unchanged H19a's 42.74%; even the impossible fixed-set selection that drops only the 66 late nonwinners and preserves the 39 late winners reaches merely **212/430 = 49.30%**. The registered capacity and negative-PnL gates pass, but the five-point win-rate gate **fails**, so no five-day-expiry native Strategy child is admitted and no alternative cutoff is searched. This is a retrospective cohort association, not a counterfactual account score: canceling an order would also alter second-tier fills, future capacity, sizing, fees and funding. The next distinct question is whether a *causally visible change in the selected support/range structure before a delayed fill* can invalidate an opportunity while retaining slow but still valid winners. It needs its own source negative case and preregistration; order age alone is not that evidence.

**Mainline custody:** D67 was first committed on the integration-commit lineage as `cca38e84e` → `ad2cf7f36` → `bfae25095`. After PR #1458 landed, the same three patches were cherry-picked without content changes onto `origin/main` `4315162e9` as `2564ac258` → `ca7453eb1` → `f04b4ec8c`; the original local branch remains intact. The main and integration trees differ only in `README.md` and `.github/workflows/quality.yml`, not in the R1 source or D67 inputs. H20a starts from this mainline continuation.

## Candidate H20a: require a completed outer-range close before B becomes a pullback anchor

**Registered before implementation, source-date gate or economic read:** After mainline migration, the published Nautilus rc3 code at `origin/main` `4315162e98cf3710a2a558325411d5e2dad94e39` still selects H19a's B by the highest **high** in the last 60 complete four-hour LAST candles; it does not require that B's **close** confirm the break. S11/S12 MATIC/FTM distinguish a completed versus weak or incomplete line break; C22 BTC warns that a small break inside a wider range is insufficient, while C20/C21 plan a *later* support pullback after a discussed break rather than a chase at B. These sources motivate a completed-break proxy but do not specify the numeric 60-bar horizontal edge, B pivot, or actual exchange order. H12's earlier 60-bar **daily immediate-entry** outer break failed; H20a is a different economic question because it keeps H19a's later 50%/61.8% **limit pullback** and existing common outside stop. Do not claim the author used this exact filter.

**One frozen rule:** Keep H19a's selected A, B-high search, two tier prices, 180 four-hour-bar order/position clock, GTD OTO brackets, target B, 0.764-plus-0.25-prior-ATR stop, 25-bp account-equity stop-risk budget, 5% per-coin notional cap and one shared account. At the **close of B's own complete four-hour bar**, before any pullback plan is created, require both `B.close > B.open` and `B.close > max(high)` of the exactly **60 complete four-hour bars immediately preceding B**. Equality or missing preceding coverage rejects that B as an anchor; do not later backfill it from future bars. When B qualifies, all H19a eligibility, supersession and native execution remain unchanged. The number 60 comes from the existing `BOX_BARS` source geometry, not a scan of H19a PnL. The rule excludes high-only wick breaks and red-candle breaks; it does not require target-room or a later retest confirmation. A source/geometry check must show completed qualifying B admitted, wick-only/red/equality/missing history rejected, and no lookahead; it must explicitly report whether the existing C20/C22 BTC source-date geometry is consistent or inconclusive instead of silently relabeling it. A BTC/ETH native pilot is an **integrity gate only**, not a coin-selection or score gate.

**Full native comparison and limits:** If source/geometry and pilot lifecycle pass, run **all 37** registered Binance USDT perpetual Catalogs from 2025-10-07 through 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC, order eligibility from 2025-10-17, one 100,000-USDT native margin account, five-minute LAST/MARK and settled funding. Run unchanged H19a using the **same changed runner/Strategy source** as a paired control, compare its native orders/fills/economics to the frozen rc3 parity report, and independently audit candidate protection, rejected/denied orders, fees and funding. Save all pilots and full attempts. Report annualized net account return, closed-position wins, payoff, Sharpe(365), daily-close drawdown, trade count, changed opportunities, fee/funding and paired week-block uncertainty. If the candidate improves only return or only win rate, report the tradeoff; do not optimize the 60-bar width, candle-body size, strictness, stop, size or expiry on this exposed year. Goal success still requires >20% annualized, approximately 60% wins and later independent forward/delayed qualification; any positive development-year result alone is unqualified. If this exact rule fails, next child should examine a structurally different support or range update mechanism rather than another close threshold.

**Pre-full native gates:** Commit `153637b1a` freezes the published-Nautilus Strategy/runner changes; source SHA-256 values are `strategy.py` `4f9425da76256f9262ce35209d487549810b5936e80f4faed9dccf1ea3d62dc6`, `broad_swing_signal.py` `937e0d9f1e9118aac59fd7b6ad48594fed983a3fe5cf09b7febbee8db66e76e6`, `tiered_retracement_strategy.py` `eb5fe402965780736cac80ff94e9800f262196ec034366c87633ebc479e9b0a5`, and `run_portfolio.py` `b48d50315cd766752dfee87fff06d9f46625765d18182447f2c25b5916edc117`. The [source/geometry gate](results/2026-10-08-h20a-source-gate.json), SHA-256 `a0e88af71a5fd785faac3048aaeb277aebcb2916758b55083d217727e54ded58`, records three passing causal synthetic cases. Exact C20/C21 author geometry remains **inconclusive**: D60's local 2026-04-10 BTC wave selects A/B **64,918.2/73,128.0** after the video's earlier approximately **67,711/72,858.5** drawing, while C22's outer-range warning has no numeric 60-bar rule. H20a proceeds only as a declared researcher proxy, not source-exact replication. The BTC/ETH native [pilot summary](results/2026-10-08-h20a-pilot-btc-eth-summary.json), SHA-256 `5a52ac0a2b81f6c2679b2221f84c68aa8038d96ed435dad3a0dffc72cec9b227`, has **74 bundles/148 OTO brackets** and **15** closed Positions; zero denied/rejected orders and `integrity_passed=true`. The [independent native audit](results/2026-10-08-h20a-pilot-btc-eth-native-audit.json), SHA-256 `317ae0cb85bcf98c47ceeb7275abf13828ab86fce6a0884e047d5e3c60f19b3a`, checks the actual original-parent entry/stop/target topology, sizes, prices, native GTD deadlines, fill netting and terminal protection and passes with no findings. The adapted auditor SHA-256 is `62401c72437ba677e85666ce947aff00540fc5b32260ab88b773a6f7e8b457b1`; it also passes unchanged 37-coin H19a (2,939 bundles/5,878 brackets/496 closed/11 open). Pilot PnL was visible but **not used** to select coins, parameters or whether to run 37; the registered next action is the full native paired experiment.

**Full 37-coin result and paired decision:** The [H20a native summary](results/2026-10-08-h20a-37-summary.json), SHA-256 `46b54ad3d3e7429dbfb38ed750653e85971977e747afd45e556c649e961f8b98`, and [same-code H19a summary](results/2026-10-08-h20a-paired-h19a-summary.json), SHA-256 `6956b80256dfb1266105a4602defefdbc8f7e8a976f67b80ba374dcb58f918a9`, both pass the native runner and independent original-parent/tier/protection audits ([H20a](results/2026-10-08-h20a-37-native-audit.json), [H19a](results/2026-10-08-h20a-paired-h19a-native-audit.json)) with zero denied/rejected orders. The [H19a published-rc3 parity read](results/2026-10-08-h20a-paired-h19a-parity.json) passes exactly on **17,665 orders, 1,722 fills, 507 Positions, and final 111,664.46988783-USDT equity**, confirming the new selector branch did not perturb the control. H20a armed **1,485** bundles versus H19a **2,939**, and closed **354** positions with **151** net winners versus **496/212**. H20a ends at **108,208.97330258 USDT**, annualized net **+8.44%**, closed win rate **42.66%**, payoff **1.602**, native Sharpe(365) **0.624**, daily-close maximum drawdown **11.24%**; H19a is **+12.00% / 42.74% / 1.608 / 0.658 / 15.33%**. H20a net account equity is **3,455.50 USDT lower**. The [native fee/funding read](results/2026-10-08-h20a-native-economics.json) finds H20a **683.78 USDT** fill commissions and **+42.80 USDT** funding adjustments versus H19a **971.32 / +46.21**, already included in their native PnL. The [356-day/52-week paired bootstrap](results/2026-10-08-h20a-paired-weekly.json), 5,000 draws with seed `20261008`, estimates daily-return annualized H20a-minus-H19a **-3.55 percentage points**, exploratory unadjusted 95% interval **[-24.47,+8.51]**, and Sharpe difference **-0.034**, interval **[-0.674,+0.640]**. These intervals cross zero and do not correct the many prior development attempts. H20a fails the Goal and shows that this completed B-close proxy discards about half the bundles **without lifting the win rate**; the lower drawdown comes with lower return. Close this exact rule, do not tune its box width, candle body or equality. The second-level mechanism should distinguish a source-relevant *effective A/support anchor* and pre-entry structural update, while testing slow but valid pullbacks as negative cases; the C20 numerical mismatch remains an explicit fidelity warning, not a parameter to fit to the viewed year.

The H20a/H19a native audit JSON SHA-256 values are `324b09e1c1669796ba6627b7f14b2cc3727dd8c2ae42b10e8ccc2c33286fdee2` / `8b2c9bfb31c074cf2ecc9987ab20609aef349b4969c41d976f43a922e654cc61`; H19a parity is `847448f90451f4d0aff8651894c91f02af507431072a57f94448cf23cfc41af0`, fee/funding read `7ac04c0c7f9f608c68c3753ab5c3d97229352c4278d32b216be5b7bc80aa19aa`, and paired weekly read `c00aca0104a5c934bb3984e9bd08608e489c01966995a953b1fb10332867358e`. Each native audit binds the raw order, fill, Position, account and return report hashes; the original `/tmp` reports remain the full readback and can be regenerated from the registered Catalog and frozen source.

**Runtime retirement after the failed result:** Commit `a61bdcaa9` reverts only H20a's selectable Strategy/runner variant and its synthetic test from the active working branch; the preregistration, frozen implementation commit `153637b1a`, original source hashes, native reports, adapted read-only auditors and finding remain in Git history. ## Diagnostic D68: can prior closed positions support a pooled causal Kelly estimate?

**Registered before the pooled time-series read:** The user proposed Kelly and variants as a high-priority position-management direction. F62-D64 show that H19a sizes each two-tier bundle at at most 25 bp of current native Portfolio equity in original stop risk and 5% of equity in coin notional, without an account-wide position-risk cap. D65's whole-account weekly bootstrap crosses zero; D66's per-coin causal sample counts are too shallow for a direct per-coin plug-in estimate. This diagnostic is a second-level extension of D66, not a new annual backtest or permission to optimize a Kelly fraction. The only outcome input is the frozen D66 native Position/OTO report `results/2026-10-08-d66-native-position-r.json.gz`, gzip SHA-256 `780da1817851913f64f5682207976f6e37d2821f5b9bb612434ac3ad36bab305`. Analyze H19a and H18a independently; their positions belong to alternative native shared-account replays and must never be combined into a fictional live sample. Literature distinguishes log-growth sizing, fractional Kelly under estimation/drawdown risk, and explicit drawdown-constrained Kelly; it does not establish Ronnie's sizing rule or a tradable R-1 edge.

**Frozen read and interpretation:** At each actual Position's first entry-order submission, include only positions of the **same run** whose native close timestamp is strictly earlier. Record prior pooled closed count, distinct coins, distinct UTC close weeks, prior pooled mean net R (native realized PnL including fees/funding divided by D66's actual gross entered stop risk), and its sign; separately retain the same-coin prior count. Preserve all right-censored open positions as unavailable outcomes. For UTC month starts from 2025-11-01 through 2026-10-01, build one prior pooled set with closes strictly before 00:00 and describe the first-order-submitted cohort of that month whose outcome is closed by the report end. For each monthly prior set containing at least four distinct ISO close weeks, resample complete prior close-week blocks 5,000 times with fixed seed `20261008`, compute the prior mean net-R 95% percentile interval, and count whether its lower bound exceeds zero. The month's later realized cohort is a *descriptive delayed check*, not an input to that month's estimate. Report sample counts, cohort mean net R, sign agreement, censoring and nonstationarity; do not scan horizons, thresholds, weights, Kelly fractions or coins. A positive lower interval in one or more months is only a lead; an interval crossing zero cannot justify positive Kelly from this single year. Both outcomes still require explicit decision-time planned-risk/overlapping-position caps and a separately preregistered native 37-coin shared-account experiment with delayed/forward qualification before any sizing claim. Position-level R ratios are not interchangeable with native whole-account log return, and an algebraic resizing of these trades is not a backtest. Continue signal/exit work in parallel because H19a's current 42.74% winning-position rate remains below the Goal.

**Completed causal read and decision:** Preregistration `0e1b2d299` preceded frozen reader `6da6cf968` (reader SHA-256 `a63ffd04161b2a92034fee3b6c83104c343d05b4e71e2b4e391eef00bf6a9d87`) and the [complete D68 result](results/2026-10-08-d68-causal-pooled-kelly.json.gz), gzip SHA-256 `67d5e9d8efde0592b78efe2d484a4d2cec601560757c797f68e6cee1e6ea4a35`. It retains decision-time pooled evidence for all **507** H19a and **686** H18a actual native Positions, reconciling **496/676** closed and retaining **11/10** open as right-censored. At the 10 monthly starts with at least four prior close weeks, **0/10 H19a** and **0/10 H18a** prior pooled mean net-R week-block intervals have a positive lower endpoint. For H19a, the May 2026 prior point mean is **+0.244 R** but its 95% exploratory interval is **[-0.216,+0.752] R**; the later May submitted cohort realizes **-0.354 R** mean. June and July likewise have positive prior point means but negative later cohort means. At October 2026 the prior sample reaches **495** closed Positions across **37** coins and **44** close weeks, yet its lower bound is still **-0.064 R**; the sole October submission remains open at report end. H18a reaches **653** prior closes across **37** coins and **45** weeks at October, interval **[-0.035,+0.305] R**. These are fixed Position risk-normalized diagnostics, not native account returns or an estimator of a tradable Kelly fraction. The exact pooled plug-in lead fails its confidence and temporal-stability check; do not add a dynamic positive Kelly size on this evidence or search a fraction on the same year. The family remains open to stronger source-conditioned advantage estimates, shrinkage/robust or risk-constrained methods, and forward evidence. A separate account-wide risk-cap diagnosis can proceed through Nautilus Portfolio/Cache and current native orders without a new ledger. It must explicitly count pending and filled stop-risk together, account for 37-coin overlap, and distinguish a risk-control benefit from the still-unmet win-rate target.

## Diagnostic D69: how much original-stop risk do simultaneous H19a pending and filled orders commit?

**Registered before constructing the combined-risk path:** This is the independent next child of F62/F67's missing account-wide risk allocation, not a Kelly-fraction fit. Use only the existing published-Nautilus rc3 H19a 37-coin shared-account report at `/tmp/nautilus-minimal-cleanup-h19a-37/`, whose original `summary.json`, `orders.csv`, `fills.csv`, and `positions.csv` SHA-256 values are respectively `d6fd06da5cb743acf568a0c80dfb6b3d45342c816977b5e5c77d0b1625a153cb`, `81c5feddd9cb44f50f6c83df921bc945b84a089bcee622fb291a7cfb1a1b8e96`, `1f338d5dcc81cfda13fa89ee164f56e6fdcfe2409f22303b53f8ae5b8fa4487f`, and `498551ad70c41701778f9b004aaa77ec2d602de72846acfd0fa3efe2cfbde218`. The [published-rc3 parity receipt](../../strategies/r1/parity-cleanup-h19a.json) binds the replay to the frozen H19a economics; the current independent native tier audit at `/tmp/r1-d69-input-native-audit.json` passes 2,939 bundles, 5,878 OTO brackets and zero findings. The Strategy's 25-bp cap is **per coin bundle**, not account-wide, and a far resting parent still represents future stop-risk capacity even when flat.

**Frozen read:** For each original BUY LIMIT entry parent, link its unique native OTO stop and native Instrument multiplier. From parent submission until terminal status or replay end, count its *unfilled remaining quantity* times `(entry limit - original stop) × multiplier` as pending planned stop risk. Subtract each actual BUY fill quantity at its native event time; remove any unfilled remainder at terminal timestamp. For every native Position, walk its own ordered `OrderFilled` events: a BUY adds `actual fill quantity × (actual fill price - original linked stop) × multiplier` to open initial-stop risk; a SELL reduces existing open risk pro rata with the quantity closed, and a still-open Position remains right-censored at replay end. This is a risk-at-original-stop proxy, not a mark-to-market loss forecast: gaps/slippage, later stop modifications and funding can change realized losses. Require exact parent/stop topology, entry quantity conservation, every entry fill matched to a Position BUY event, Position closing quantity conservation, no negative running risk, and reconciliation of native filled-parent/Position counts. Aggregate pending, open and combined risk at every native order/fill event over the **same** 2025-10-17 00:00 UTC to 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC eligible window. Report time-weighted mean, median, p95 and peak USD risk, peak timestamp, share from pending, and bps of the original 100,000-USDT account only; do not label those bps as live Portfolio-equity-normalized risk. Keep the exact full path or event deltas, including right-censored terminal state, for independent readback. Do not resize orders, simulate counterfactual PnL, or select a cap from this exposed path. The decision is whether an account-wide cap is a real missing control, separate from the observed 42.74% win-rate deficit; any later cap candidate must first be frozen as one rule and replayed through native Strategy/Risk/Execution/Portfolio with all 37 coins, fees, funding, no denied/rejected orders and paired/future qualification.

**Retained reader attempts before accepted evidence:** Reader freeze `8a1a3546f` first failed before path construction because the native starting balance is serialized as a string rather than a JSON number. Repair `4b2c16ac0` passed that gate, then failed on `1E-25` Decimal rounding residue after a fully closed two-tier Position; repair `099326e60` accepts only residues below `1E-8` while still rejecting unmatched quantity or material risk. That reader produced [attempt 1](results/2026-10-08-d69-combined-stop-risk-attempt1.json.gz), gzip SHA-256 `6a1b2b5a17a72ed4510075a7c6ddf01c1aa7bd7ac77cc666b06d707a51e8c5b7`, but its reported positive-parent count was falsely **5,878**: a `defaultdict` lookup created zero-filled keys while reconciling all parents. The observed risk path is retained as a failed read, not selected evidence. The next reader correction uses a non-mutating lookup and requires 881 positive parents; no order, stop or Portfolio facts are changed.

**Accepted D69 path and decision:** Repair commit `ef4fa2809` freezes reader SHA-256 `0caeb2428030778e786ff15d8d1aad3353a139d22bb05b30b39938ede5192c0b`; its [complete original-stop risk path](results/2026-10-08-d69-combined-stop-risk.json.gz), gzip SHA-256 `5406e4de8387d006417c8ac5bbac485edd9c698547a8e731ba5c5a102361b8b9`, was byte-for-byte reproduced. It reconciles **17,665** native orders, **5,878** entry OTO parents, **881** positively filled entry parents, **1,722** native fills, **507** Positions (**496** closed and **11** right-censored open), with no missing trade IDs, quantity mismatch or negative risk. Across the **355.354-day** common eligibility window, time-weighted original-stop commitments average **2,936.23 USDT pending** plus **2,103.64 USDT held**, combined **5,039.87 USDT**; pending represents **58.26%** of the combined mean. The combined time-weighted median/p95 are **5,512.53 / 8,979.79 USDT**; peak **9,863.15 USDT** at 2026-10-05 00:04:59.999 UTC. These equal **504.0 bps mean / 986.3 bps peak of the fixed original 100,000-USDT balance**, *not* of evolving Portfolio equity. At the replay end, **7,787.61 USDT** pending and **2,037.52 USDT** open original-stop proxy remain right-censored. An independent reread of all 2,523 event intervals confirms exact pending + open = combined, nonnegative risk, full period coverage and the time-weighted means. Thus the 25-bp **per bundle** risk cap demonstrably does not bound simultaneous account commitments to 25 bp; there is material account-wide overlap, particularly from resting orders. This establishes a missing control surface, not the counterfactual benefit of a particular cap or a Kelly estimate. The next rule must say how to allocate and release capacity among pending/filled opportunities and freeze a risk limit from a declared risk objective, not choose it by scanning this exposed path. Later native backtests must report both return and win-rate consequences, costs/funding, drawdown and opportunity displacement.

## Candidate H21a: replace the broad wave's old low with the latest decision-confirmed effective A

H14a's standalone 50%-only/1.5R and H15a's three-tier/below-origin-stop/30-bar economics do **not** test this two-tier/180-bar/76.4-stop H19a hybrid. C20's old-low or rising-line structural stop may be wider than H19a's fixed below-76.4-plus-0.25-prior-ATR stop; source geometry fit cannot silently certify stop fidelity. The source corpus, H14a source gate and D60 source diagnostic have SHA-256 `ecb2bedcd007b83eee8ac2094d7d36ed97290317c434ed5c09d529eb35da607f`, `99a7280b6fd5f187fc626dedf1c96092bfb18da206f0b9b7c213c5c9630f8084`, and `c4e653dd6647edb66b8e9bf987df568ff968af1c652f26abf2ae71456ee397ba`. The pre-candidate native broad selector/Strategy/runner SHA-256 values are `c8dcb097b924dc90339207bf3f751fc88b3b95acf9512ba3689ab62e4e7b57cd`, `f43cd4603794cb68232168c23c0739427c979e1f90ad6f76460d2d21a06935fc`, and `cbe3f6c25a7e937d79723d36c99b5653ea8fe30a2b02b7ca515a4ed6709b04f0`.

**One frozen mechanism and source gates:** Retain H19a's B as the earliest maximum high of the last 60 complete four-hour LAST bars, order-8 low pivots, 180-bar A lookback measured backward from B, at least six bars A-before-B span, and all bars' untouched-50% eligibility. At each **completed decision bar i**, allow an A low with `A+8 <= i` even when confirmation occurred *after* B; among eligible lows choose the **latest index** rather than the absolute lowest low. No pivot is backdated to a decision before its confirmation. If this newly confirmed A changes the pair while an old same-B bundle is still fully unfilled, retire its native entry orders and wait for cancellation before submitting the updated pair; keep an actually filled older position and its native protection unchanged. All native 50%/61.8% limit tiers, below-76.4-plus-0.25-prior-ATR common stop, B target, 180-four-hour-bar GTD and position clock, equal 25-bp total Portfolio-equity stop-risk budget, 5% total coin notional cap, and one shared account stay as H19a. No wider-stop, source pixel, line, breakout threshold, pivot-order, lifetime or Kelly-fraction sweep is admitted. Synthetic cases must show confirmation happens only at A+8, an older A is replaced after the local pivot becomes confirmed, no replacement after any fill, no same-event backfill, and the untouched/OTO cancel-race rules. A read-only native BTC source-date check must find the selected C20 local A/B within the already frozen A `[67,500,68,000]` and B `[72,500,73,000]` bands, with 50%/61.8%/76.4% inside D31's frozen tier bands, and a causally armed untouched 50% plan at **at least one** of the already frozen 2026-04-09 00:00/04:00 UTC cutoffs. It must report both cutoffs, the later S44 broad-wave geometry, C22's earlier unfinished local impulse, and any source mismatch without selecting a favorable time. The S44 broad source fit is a scale countercase, not an H21a gate: this rule explicitly tests the C20 local wave rather than claiming one selector fits both scales. Failure of the C20 source gate stops the native economic test for this exact H21a rule; do not adjust the pivot order or time cutoff on the seen chart.

**Frozen economic test if source gate passes:** First run an exact BTC/ETH shared-account Nautilus integrity pilot, checking accepted OTO topology, native original stop and target, native cancellation acknowledgments and race fills, zero denied/rejected, and right-censored open protection. Pilot PnL does not choose whether to run the full pool. Then replay the **same 37 registered perpetual coins** with existing 2025-10-07 to 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC five-minute LAST/MARK/funding Catalogs, 2025-10-17 eligibility, one native 100,000-USDT margin account, and a same-code unchanged H19a control. Report outcome tradeoffs and all prior Goal attempts; no in-year parameter revision or selected coin subset can qualify an advantage, and delayed/forward evidence remains required. If the local-A mechanism fails, investigate whether the source's wider structural invalidation or range-position context explains the mismatch as a separately registered second-level child, rather than abandoning effective-A research or tuning A's pivot size on this year.

**Source and causal geometry gate:** Native candidate/reader freeze `7a6c2c209` has broad-selector SHA-256 `54fe5b2eb7eb62d3e4859dd2158181e6c5bc0651cdfd5130bd5b92cd78d03d19`, tiered Strategy `47a12135b2005bbc4650e6854ac42d871d5dea61a9e9a8a519a71d29d4f4ac10`, base Strategy `09a9732fa674cda2cad56c2b522a37a7276d0b8e03fcfacd890a72d763fa0fc0`, runner `fb32eb2cb4dad4b237a77b472ed6bdf6755fb5906f040e5fbcfa002e772def08`, and read-only source reader `6ecc972f2df9ddd4a578c1bfd573174bd439f8203592d01d224515563f4b6264`. ## Diagnostic D70: how much native margin balance is reserved while stop-risk commitments coexist?

**Registered before reading the full account path:** Use the frozen H19a same-code 37-coin native `account.csv` from `/tmp/r1-h21a-paired-h19a-37` and the already frozen D69 stop-risk audit; do not run a sizing counterfactual or select a new risk cap. From the native account's `total`, `locked`, and `free` USDT balance columns, keep the last native account report at each timestamp, carry that state only forward to the next report, and time-weight over the same 2025-10-17 00:00 to 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC trade window. Report mean and maximum `locked`, time-weighted `locked/total` and `free/total`, minimum `free`, and account-row invariants (`total = locked + free`, nonnegative balances if true). Compare their scale to D69's **5,039.87-USDT mean** and **9,863.15-USDT peak** simultaneous original-stop-risk commitments without equating margin balance with mark-to-market equity, economic capital, or guaranteed risk. Preserve the native account CSV hash and explicit interpretation; no Kelly fraction or strategy performance claim follows from this diagnostic. A source-confirmed account-level policy must separately decide whether pending orders reserve capacity, whether closer opportunities replace farther ones, and how a causal edge estimator and drawdown bound constrain a budget. Use Nautilus Portfolio/Cache and native order lifecycle as the implementation owner; no parallel capital ledger.

**Read and decision:** The first read-only parser attempt rejected mixed native timestamp precision before producing an artifact; `format="mixed"` fixed only CSV timestamp decoding. [D70's native account read](results/2026-10-08-d70-native-margin-occupancy.json), SHA-256 `e44518497f20fb1498b38231223463670c18d6aab314736a84451be1e87d1946`, binds the same-code H19a account CSV SHA-256 `69530413f22af40f57c5290dcc06417580c9f95f395b0c7c465b1d95ac2ab33e`. Its 3,678 distinct native account timestamps satisfy `total = locked + free` within **1.46e-11 USDT**, with no negative balances. Over the registered trade window, time-weighted locked margin balance is **7,704.20 USDT**, mean `locked/total` **7.93%**, mean `free/total` **92.07%**; maximum locked is **16,244.27 USDT**, minimum free **77,682.79 USDT**. D69's simultaneous original-stop-risk commitment is a different unit: **5,039.87** mean and **9,863.15 USDT** peak, including pending and filled original stop distances. Thus the account often has ample *native free margin*, while many concurrent native orders already promise meaningful potential stop losses. The low locked fraction is evidence that margin reservation is not the immediate mechanical limit on H19a order submissions; it does not show that multiplying every position is safe or that annual return should be divided by locked margin. Annual net return remains measured against the full 100,000-USDT shared account. Without a stable positive causal edge estimate (D68), no positive Kelly multiplier is admitted. Next sizing design must make account-level pending-plus-open risk, correlations, opportunity priority and drawdown bound explicit, and test any single preregistered rule through the same Nautilus account and paired native replay; sizing alone cannot bring a fixed trade set's win rate near 60%.

## Candidate H22a: put the local effective A's common stop outside A

**Registration before implementation, source-date read and economic replay:** H21a selected the C20/C21 local A/B at the already frozen April 9 00:00/04:00 UTC cutoffs, but its common stop remained only below the 76.4% retracement. H21a's 37-coin economics worsened to -6.18% annual/36.78% wins versus H19a +12.00%/42.74%, while H15a's different three-tier/below-origin/shorter-life candidate reached 56.51% wins but only +4.28% annual. This is disclosed exposed-year development context, not independent evidence that a new stop will succeed. Test exactly one second-level child of the failed local-A mechanism: retain H21a's decision-confirmed **latest** order-8 A, same-B unfilled native bundle replacement, earliest highest B of the last 60 completed four-hour bars, 180-bar B-backward A eligibility and six-bar A-to-B span, untouched 50% rule, 50%/61.8% equal risk tiers, B target, 180-four-hour-bar GTD/hold, one 25-bp Portfolio-equity stop-risk budget, 5% coin-notional cap, 37 coins and one native account. Change only the common stop to **A low minus 0.25 times the prior completed four-hour ATR**. Native Instrument rounds stop and levels; reject nonpositive or nonordered protection, never place an order without a native stop. The 0.25 ATR buffer is inherited from H19a/H21a, not fitted here. Do not sweep ATR fraction, pivot size, A window, tier weights, order age, capital multiplier or individual coins on this exposed year.

**Gates and falsifiers:** From the same frozen registered Binance perpetual LAST Catalog and previously fixed C20 cutoffs, require A/B and untouched 50% eligibility still match H21a's source read; the candidate stop must be strictly below A, 76.4%, both intended tier entries and target, with both entries below the decision close. Record the exact candidate stop and planned first/deeper tier B-target reward/original-stop-risk ratios at both cutoffs, plus S44's known broader-wave mismatch; failure of any C20 gate stops this exact candidate before economics, with no date/threshold retry. Exercise published `nautilus_trader==2.0.0rc3` native order lifecycle for target, stop, second-tier fill, same-bar stop, 180-bar expiry/time exit, same-B replacement and cancellation-race fill, then a real-Catalog BTC/ETH shared-account integrity pilot. Its incidental PnL cannot determine whether to run the complete pool. If all execution gates pass, run all registered 37 coins from 2025-10-07 through 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC with eligibility from 2025-10-17, five-minute LAST/MARK/funding, 100,000-USDT one margin account. Pair against same-code H21a (isolating the stop change) and H19a (current champion); require H19a order/fill/Position/equity exact parity against the frozen published-rc3 control. Audit every native OTO/protected open, rejected/denied orders, fees/funding, full net equity, closed win/payoff, Sharpe(365), daily-close drawdown, count and account-risk shifts. Pair daily returns by the same registered ISO-week bootstrap (5,000 draws, seed 20261008) and preserve all attempts. A point-estimate improvement or even joint Goal score on this heavily exposed annual development set is not independent validation; multiplicity-aware delayed/forward evidence is still required. If the wider A stop reduces stopouts but cuts risk-adjusted return or opportunity capacity, investigate whether the missing larger-range context rather than further stop-width tuning is the next source-driven child.
