H26a clears the predeclared 25-additional-closure breadth check and stays below H19a's drawdown ceiling, but **fails** annual net >20% and the 55%-65% win gate; stop this child after this one annual candidate. An increase of final equity by **1,142.11 USDT** versus H25a on this already viewed year is an exploratory point result, not independent evidence of an edge.

**Unverified explanation and next smallest experiment:** The working explanation is that admitting tested A support despite a prior B test increases valid entry flow while adding enough nonwinning B-obstructed or weak reversals to lower win rate and Sharpe. The observed 30/68 added wins and lower candidate payoff are consistent with it but do **not** prove the B pivot blocked price; other account-path, price and censoring changes remain possible. The next separate registration should be a **read-only native path attribution** of the 482 added source bundles: identify actual B-target versus stop/time exits, pre-order B pivot geometry, price/age paths for winners including slow starters, and nine open censoring; decide whether an actionable condition exists before any new Strategy rule. Do not retroactively pick a B-distance threshold, coin subset or second annual variant from this result.

## Diagnostic D92: did prior B resistance actually obstruct H26a's added native opportunities?

**Registered before granular H26a path read:** Bind the completed H26a 37-coin native summary SHA-256 `cc8f4df0199cb6c1b16146af95b880461b11da8e8ca2dd0775ff59ef59b9912a`, native protection audit `164f41a06fbb3f401511142ebf759c84634dde6b4853fd12c36939a4371a98df`, independent structural clock audit `91edf5383a81758a17010b9e24efcdfd78b547ea94ab9f56230331c108121d5e`, H26a/H25a source-key flow `068073fd694adbfa520d109afca61d2ba348a0888e00f58a8cddaa373c8077cc`, and registered Catalog identity `ce9963ca68c66d34af74dbdbfff484f320a622fa73afe64844518aed354ec9fc`. The population is **all 482 H26a-only submitted source bundles** (same coin, native stop, native B target and original signal clock), including unfilled, filled, closed and any censored open state. The already reported 68 candidate-only closed Positions/30 positive are prior knowledge, not an independent discovery. H25a's 655 common-source bundles serve only as a descriptive control under a different account path. Do not select coins, months or a profitable subset.

**Frozen read-only mechanism and counterexamples:** First reconcile each H26a-only native Position to its actual opening parent, second-tier fills, native closing order type/tag, original protective stop, B target, first fill price/time, realized PnL including native fees/funding, and source structural decision. Recompute earlier confirmed B swing highs and the prior ATR from verified *completed* four-hour LAST Catalog bars, recording the nearest previously tested B level's distance to rounded B in units of prior ATR; the source signal bar may not confirm its own older pivot. This tests the claimed `prior B` state without fitting a new distance. From the first real BUY fill use initial risk `first fill price − original native stop` and the original B room `B − first fill price`; require both positive or report integrity failure. For price paths use only whole completed four-hour bars **strictly after the first-fill-containing bar and strictly before the native exit-containing bar**; omit partial entry/exit bars rather than infer intrabar event order. Report MFE and MAE divided by initial R, and the maximum fraction of the original first-fill-to-B room reached. At the **first 1/3/5/10 complete post-fill four-hour bars**, report path state only for positions still open after that bar's completion; separately count positions already closed or end-censored at each landmark. Native B-target, stop and time exits remain authoritative even when the strict interior-bar path is empty. Report first/second native tier fill effects, monthly/coin concentration and losing paths that reached at least half of the B room, those that never reached half, and late eventual winners that had no positive progress by an early landmark. Half-room and the 1/3/5/10 landmarks are fixed descriptive checkpoints, not entry/exit thresholds.

**Interpretation and stop boundary:** If most added native losers never reached half the B room before exit, the specific claim that old B resistance blocked them is weak; an earlier loss or invalidation mechanism deserves the next question. If many came close to B and reversed, retain B obstruction only as a plausible hypothesis because an old pivot's causal effect cannot be established from one viewed cohort. Any slow eventual winner after weak early progress contradicts a blanket time exit. This is **one read-only diagnostic**, with no hypothetical fills, exit PnL, Strategy edit, account replay, threshold sweep or promotion claim. On completion, record raw counts, counterexamples, data-clock caveats, and the next smallest separately registered experiment; keep annual H26a results exploratory and demand delayed/forward validation for any eventual improvement.

**D92 completed native read:** The [summary](results/2026-10-09-d92-h26a-b-obstacle.json), SHA-256 `c49f090ec671f0799b4ef74d8fc34c520b82903238cfda470514b73770826937`, and [all 482 source-bundle paths](results/2026-10-09-d92-h26a-b-obstacle-bundles.json.gz), SHA-256 `46b929aefe317d1e16ab73eb7ea389c96684ba40f6fd880969c4e677d78c20ab`, bind the native reports and independently verified 37 LAST Catalog trees. **415/482** new submitted bundles made no Position; the other 67 source bundles produced **68** native Positions, all closed, with **30 B-target final exits and 38 stop final exits**. Every one of the 482 sources had a causally confirmed prior B test by the frozen pivot/ATR rule, with nearest old B pivot a median **0.394 prior ATR** from B. First actual fill had median target room **1.684 initial R**. Of the 38 nonpositive native closed Positions, **27** reached **less than half** the original first-fill-to-B room across all strictly interior complete four-hour bars, **nine** reached at least half, and **two** had no complete interior bar. This is a lower-bound observation because partial entry/exit bars were excluded. The preregistered “most losses reverse near old B” interpretation is **not supported** by these paths; many losses developed well before the B area. This does not prove that prior B has no effect.

The deeper tier is an important **selected-path warning**: **52/68** native Positions had both distinct entry parents fill, including **36 stops and 16 targets**; among the 16 single-entry Positions, **two stopped and 14 targeted**. The first cohort is reached only after a deeper adverse price path and is not randomized evidence that the second order caused the loss. A blanket early-exit rule also conflicts with native winners: among still-open Positions, **15** eventual winners closed their first complete four-hour bar at/below the first fill, **11** did so at bar three, **nine** at bar five and **six** at bar ten. Source-month results vary sharply: July 2026 had eight closed/zero positive, September three/three positive; individual coin groups are sparse and cannot set a coin filter. Actual native realized Position PnL includes fees/funding; R-normalized Catalog paths do not. No new account equity or counterfactual exit return was manufactured. The B-obstacle hypothesis is weakened, and the next separately registered child should ask whether the **timing of second-tier fills versus already weakening evidence** can be identified from completed bars without discarding eventual two-tier winners. No immediate second-tier cancellation or one-tier annual candidate follows from this association.

## Diagnostic D93: is completed-bar evidence available before the deeper native tier fills?

**Pre-registration before second-fill chronology read:** D92's frozen summary SHA-256 `c49f090ec671f0799b4ef74d8fc34c520b82903238cfda470514b73770826937` and all-source detail SHA-256 `46b929aefe317d1e16ab73eb7ea389c96684ba40f6fd880969c4e677d78c20ab` establish the 482 added H26a-only source bundles, 68 native Positions, and selected 52 two-tier fills. Ronnie C18/C20 treats 50% and 61.8% as alternative possible support entries with a common outside stop; this source does not authorize canceling the second tier after a weak candle. H18a earlier tested a *different* confirmed-line break cancellation. Bind exact H26a and same-code H25a native orders/fills/Positions, D92 Catalog identity and both passing audits. Keep H26a-only as the focal population; H25a's common-source Positions are a descriptive check for whether the timing pattern is peculiar to the added B-test state, not a matched causal control.

**Frozen clock and comparison:** For every actual native Position, identify its first and second **distinct** BUY parent fill times from native Position events and both original OTO parent IDs. For two-tier Positions, place the second fill relative to the four-hour bar containing the first fill: (a) in that same incomplete bar, (b) after it but before the first fully post-fill four-hour bar completes, or (c) only after at least one full post-fill bar completed. Count native stop/target/time final exits, positive fee/funding-inclusive Position outcomes and first-to-second delay in each state; retain later tier partial fills separately. When state (c) exists, read **only the last four-hour LAST bar completed strictly before second fill** and report its close relative to first fill and preconfirmed rounded A, plus elapsed complete-bar count. For states (a)/(b), report `no_completed_post_fill_decision` and do not project an unavailable signal. Preserve native final winner counterexamples with a weak latest complete close and slow winners from D92; no percentage-body or ATR-distance sweep. Recompute coin Catalog identity and the complete-bar clock; do not inspect later bars to assign an earlier signal.

**Interpretation and budget:** If a majority of actual second-tier fills occurs with no complete post-fill four-hour bar available, a four-hour-bar conditional cancellation cannot intercept most observed second fills and this branch stops before native candidate work. If a substantial later-fill population exists but weak-bar state is shared by eventual target winners, no simple no-progress gate is justified. Even a separated state would require a new source-based rule, protection/race audit and preregistered same-account native replay; D93 alone estimates no counterfactual cashflows or annual return. Exactly **one** read-only pass over these frozen runs/Catalogs, with all 37 coins and no date/coin exclusion. The entire interval and D92 population are already exposed; this result is not independent validation.

**D93 complete chronology and counterexamples:** The [frozen native clock report](results/2026-10-09-d93-second-tier-clock.json), SHA-256 `330e0e5908d3a6b2577b94a0815a0b9042844de84a2c962926ddf24e02b15930`, and [per-Position fill chronology](results/2026-10-09-d93-second-tier-clock-positions.json.gz), SHA-256 `1505b7689a78149108428959f9549e5170caf6a34ee2927224b3db450859523c`, verify the same 37 Catalog trees and H26a/H25a native report identities. Among the **52 H26a-only two-tier Positions**, **eight** second parents filled in the first fill's incomplete four-hour bar, **eight** before the first fully post-fill bar completed, and **36** only after at least one full post-fill bar. Those 36 had median **25.04 hours / five complete bars** between first and second entry; **27** ultimately stopped and **nine** hit B. Thus the preregistered majority-too-early gate **did not fire**: a four-hour decision clock existed before most second fills. However, among those 36 later entries, **24** had a last completed close at/below their first fill, including **five actual eventual native winners**. **Zero** had a last completed close below the originally confirmed rounded A support. A naive weak-close or A-break cancellation is not supported by this source state.

The H25a common-source descriptive check has **79 two-tier Positions**, **65** with a completed post-first-fill bar before the second entry; these had median **59.08 hours / 14 complete bars** and **28 eventual positive native closes**, with **23 winners among the 52** whose last completed close remained at/below first fill. It also has nine open Positions overall; its time-market exits and opens are kept distinct from target/stop outcomes. This counterexample shows that a weak completed close is common even when previously tested A support has **no** prior B test; it cannot be promoted by selecting H26a-only losses. No second-parent partial-fill event changed the clock classification. The late-fill opportunity exists, but neither A invalidation nor the simple weak-close proxy separates it reliably. Keep the native two-tier Strategy unchanged; the next step should obtain a **source-grounded structural invalidation** for a different, genuinely observable price-action state (for example, a previously drawn support line or market-cycle boundary) and test its causal availability against both losers and slow winners before registering an economic candidate. Reusing D93's 36 stops to fit that line or pick coins would contaminate the already viewed year further.

## Diagnostic D94: does the native 76.4%-buffer stop invalidate prior A support before A fails?

**Pre-registered source tension and mechanism:** Ronnie C02 says a structural stop sits outside the chosen support zone and a wick alone does not break support; C12 distinguishes an already broken horizontal band from a still-intact lower low; C20 discusses a common stop below a rising line or prior low for multiple retracement entries. C18 also explicitly permits a stop below **76.4%**, so it is not valid to claim the author always places stops below the wide A pivot. H25a/H26a admit plans because a prior confirmed swing low near A was tested, while the current native selector sets `stop = B − 0.764*(B−A) − 0.25*prior_ATR`. Algebraically its stop lies above A whenever `0.236*(B−A) > 0.25*prior_ATR`; the source-relevant question is how often native Positions actually stop while that preconfirmed A region remains unbroken. This is an **encoding and risk-geometry diagnosis**, not an assertion that a wider stop improves returns or that A is the author's exact zone.

**Frozen population, data and read:** Bind same-code H25a/H26a 37-coin native summary/orders/fills/Positions and passing tier/clock audits, H26a/H25a exact source-key flow, 37 registered five-minute LAST/MARK/funding Catalog trees and existing identity SHA-256 `ce9963ca68c66d34af74dbdbfff484f320a622fa73afe64844518aed354ec9fc`. Preserve **all** 655 H25a and 1,137 H26a submitted source bundles, their actual filled/closed/open outcomes and overlapping keys; do not infer new account PnL from either subset. At each accepted source decision use its rounded A/B, original native stop and **prior** Wilder ATR; classify the native stop above/equal/below A and report `(stop−A)/ATR` plus old first/deeper-tier target-to-stop R. As a frozen *geometric illustration only*, derive one-tick-rounded `A − 0.25*prior_ATR` as an outside-A protective price and its first/deeper-tier target-to-stop R, invalid/nonpositive cases, and notional implication at the unchanged 25-bp risk/5% cap; submit no order. For every actual native stop-closed Position, use its opening/closing fill events and verified native five-minute LAST bars to separate (1) any A touch strictly before the exit-containing bar, (2) A touch in the exit-containing bar where intrabar ordering is uncertain, and (3) no A touch even in that bar. Keep native exit price relative to A and multiple-tier fills separate. Check 5-minute clock continuity and Catalog digest; do not substitute MARK for LAST execution or claim an unseen book fill.

**Decision and contamination boundary:** A large count of native stop exits with no LAST A touch through the exit bar supports the narrower statement that the current stop often cuts exposure *before its admission support is breached*; it does not prove holding through A would win. If stop-above-A is rare, or most stopped paths also touch A in/before the exit bar, retire this avenue. If the geometric target room after an outside-A stop is too small under 25-bp risk and 5% cap, report that tradeoff rather than increasing leverage or choosing a different buffer on this exposed year. Only if source semantics, viable geometry and actual stop-before-A incidence jointly support it may a **separately registered single native Strategy child** use a common outside-A stop, unchanged entry/target and native sizing/protection in one 37-coin paired account replay; no new native candidate is authorized by D94 itself. Report winners, slow paths, fee/funding, coin/month breadth and nine H25a/H26a opens as counterexamples or censoring. This is one read-only pass; all market outcomes remain development-exposed and any future annual improvement requires multiplicity-aware delayed/forward validation.

**D94 native geometry and limit:** The [summary](results/2026-10-09-d94-stop-vs-a.json), SHA-256 `a8b9c407a22dfb52d0f5492cbb2fdfe3817abfc642a7f1c720c3e2a339a7e6ad`, and [all source bundles and actual stop exits](results/2026-10-09-d94-stop-vs-a-bundles.json.gz), SHA-256 `f5f68595fb47f10448db5944a89fbf7e079f64a9fa01289506364685dbd5b713`, verify 37 contiguous five-minute native LAST Catalog trees and exact H25a/H26a report identities. **All 655/655 H25a and 1,137/1,137 H26a submitted bundles** placed their actual native stop **above** the rounded A low; median distance was **2.10** and **1.93 prior ATR**, respectively. Of **47 H25a final stop exits, 45** showed no A touch even in the exit-containing LAST bar; of **85 H26a final stop exits, 82** did not. The remaining two/three touched A in the same exit bar, where event ordering is ambiguous; no stop-exit fill price was at/below A. The original support admission therefore often remains unbroken when the native position is stopped. It does not follow that a later A test would recover or that the earlier 76.4%-buffer stop is always source-inconsistent: C18 separately permits that level.

At unchanged entry/B and a *hypothetical* `A − 0.25 prior ATR` stop, all submitted bundle prices remain valid, but the H26a median first-tier planned B room compresses from **1.718R to 0.949R**, deeper tier from **3.574R to 1.511R**. Keeping the same stop-risk currency allocation alone would scale median first-tier quantity to **0.552** of the old quantity before the 5% notional cap, quantity increments or a changed Portfolio path. H25a shows nearly identical geometry (first **1.728R to 0.952R**, quantity ratio **0.551**). These are read-only price/risk ratios, *not* alternative native fills, wins, funding or Account PnL. This jointly supports one testable structural-stop mechanism and a serious capacity/reward counterforce. No threshold was selected from the 45/82 exits.

## Candidate H27a: H26a prior-A-support plans with the common stop outside A

**Single candidate registered before Strategy edits or results:** Keep H26a's exact prior-A-support admission regardless of B history, 37 frozen Binance USDⓈ-M instruments, one 100,000-USDT Nautilus native margin account, five-minute LAST/MARK/funding Catalogs, 2025-10-07 input start, 2025-10-17 order eligibility and 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC end. Keep 50%/61.8% LIMIT parents, B target, 180-four-hour-bar pending/hold lifecycle, native OTO sibling order behavior, 25-bp **total per bundle** original-stop risk and 5%-of-equity per-coin notional cap. Change **only** the protective stop of an accepted native plan to one instrument-rounded tick representation of `rounded A − 0.25 * prior 14-bar Wilder ATR`, using the ATR known *before* its source decision candle. Require `0 < new stop < rounded A < both entries < B`; otherwise suppress the entire bundle and report the skip. Do not change tier weights, A/B selector, pivot order, ATR period, stop buffer, order validity, time exit, coins or risk budget. This is a researcher translation of C02/C20's outside-support stop, not a claim that Ronnie always uses the broad A selected by H19a; C18's 76.4%-stop source case remains a contrary acceptable substyle. A broader stop changes native equity sizing, fills, funding, opportunity flow and target R; old Position outcomes must not be reused as H27 outcomes.

**Falsification, legal controls and budget:** The mechanism predicts fewer exits while A remains intact and a higher native closed win rate, but the preregistered counterforce is the about **45%** first-tier quantity reduction and B room below 1R. Refute a useful account-level extension if annualized net return does not exceed H26a's **+14.3053%**, if win rate remains below the operational **55%-65%** near-60% gate, if payoff/Sharpe degrade materially, or if protection/causal ATR/a 25-bp or 5% bound fails. Goal success still requires annualized net **strictly above 20%**, closed win near 60% and then high Sharpe, with drawdown reported; H19a's **15.33%** daily-close drawdown is the predeclared ceiling. First run **one** BTC/ETH native integrity pilot with an independent completed-bar stop-formula audit; pilot PnL never chooses the year. If integrity passes, run **one** full 37-coin H27a native shared-account replay and **one each** same-code H26a and H25a controls, requiring frozen order/fill/Position/equity parity before interpretation. Report native fees/funding, final equity, annual net, closed win/payoff, Sharpe365, daily-close drawdown, submitted/filled/closed/open, rejected/denied, source-plan overlap, time-weighted Account locked margin and paired ISO-week uncertainty. If the full run fails the two Goal gates, stop this child and attribute lost profit to smaller risk-sized exposure, different stops, targets and native slot changes; do not scan buffers, add a third tier or raise leverage on the exposed year. Even an apparent annual pass is development evidence after many prior trials; delayed/forward independent validation and multiplicity accounting remain required for promotion.

**H27a run and native facts (after the preceding registration):** Inventory of this working tree found `research/r1_native/RD_EXPERIMENTS.md`, `SOURCE_CASES.md`, `results/`, native replay and audit scripts in `strategies/r1/`; no `research/records` tool or directory is present. The implementation was committed as `2dd821cb2` before any H27a native run; the [artifact manifest](results/2026-10-09-h27a-manifest.json) binds preregistration, implementation, run windows and all 15 committed report hashes. A single full-year BTC/ETH integrity pilot passed [native OTO/tier audit](results/2026-10-09-h27a-btc-eth-native-audit.json) and [independent completed-bar stop clock](results/2026-10-09-h27a-btc-eth-support-clock.json); its [summary](results/2026-10-09-h27a-btc-eth-summary.json) was not used to choose the annual candidate. The one 37-coin native shared-account [candidate summary](results/2026-10-09-h27a-37-summary.json), SHA-256 `38df405e9df35a98e526888428afb65630075da636a23317d710908199e53f6a`, passed [native lifecycle, 25-bp/5% sizing and protection audit](results/2026-10-09-h27a-37-native-audit.json) and [independent A/ATR causal clock audit](results/2026-10-09-h27a-37-support-clock.json): **1,073** submitted two-tier native bundles, **282** filled entries, **151** closed Positions, **nine** open/censored, zero denied/rejected orders. On the one 100,000-USDT account, final native equity was **107,685.43 USDT**, annualized net **+7.9021%**, closed win **94/151 = 62.25%**, mean positive/mean nonpositive Position net PnL **0.951**, native Sharpe365 **1.200**, daily-close max drawdown **4.628%**. These are account/Position facts, including fill commissions and recorded funding adjustments, not hypothetical individual order returns.

**Legal controls and costs:** Same-code H26a and H25a replays each passed native audits and exact frozen order/fill/Position/equity [H26a parity](results/2026-10-09-h27a-control-h26a-parity.json) / [H25a parity](results/2026-10-09-h27a-control-h25a-parity.json). The [H26a control](results/2026-10-09-h27a-control-h26a-summary.json) gave **113,902.11 USDT**, **+14.3053%**, **85/170 = 50.00%** win, payoff **1.754**, Sharpe365 **1.552**, drawdown **4.685%**; the [H25a control](results/2026-10-09-h27a-control-h25a-summary.json) gave **112,759.99 USDT**, **+13.1282%**, **55/102 = 53.92%** win, Sharpe365 **1.905**, drawdown **3.068%**. The [native fill/Position economics readback](results/2026-10-09-h27a-vs-h26a-economics.json) attributes H27a **177.92 USDT** fill commissions and **−120.10 USDT** Position funding adjustments, against H26a **338.83** and **−267.19** respectively; these are included in realized net Positions. H27a ended **52** Positions by native STOP_LOSS (**−12,881.40 USDT** aggregate closed PnL), **87** by TAKE_PROFIT (**+19,759.41**) and **12** by market time exit (**+735.68**), versus H26a **85** stops (**−18,080.37**), **80** targets (**+29,707.25**) and **five** market exits (**+2,010.81**). Year-end open marks make exit-group PnL sums differ from final account equity.

**Flow and uncertainty:** The [read-only source-key/Account flow](results/2026-10-09-h27a-vs-h26a-flow.json) matched **1,071** submitted A/B/clock source keys; H27a had **two** unique and H26a **66** unique after the shared-account paths diverged. On those common source keys, H27a had **159** Positions (**150** closed, **94** positive, **+7,871.17 USDT** closed realized PnL), H26a **163** (**155** closed, **76** positive, **+11,091.53**). This is descriptive, because stop/sizing/slot and year-end censoring differ. Time-weighted native Account locked/total was **1.864%** H27a vs **2.746%** H26a; it measures locked margin, not gross notional, total equity usage or unrealized risk. The [paired ISO-week analysis](results/2026-10-09-h27a-vs-h26a-paired.json) observed annualized daily-return difference **−6.39 percentage points**, exploratory 95% week-bootstrap interval **[−16.77,+0.76] pp**, and Sharpe365 difference **−0.352** with interval **[−1.001,+0.221]**. Intervals are unadjusted for the many prior annual tries and cross zero; they do not certify a population advantage or disadvantage.

**Decision, limits and next smallest experiment:** The predeclared H27a account-extension test **fails**: annualized net falls below H26a and the strict >20% Goal despite a near-60% win rate; payoff, Sharpe and average locked margin also fall. Fewer stops and more target closes are native facts. The proposed explanation that a wider structural stop suppresses winners' currency size and opportunity flow is **plausible but not isolated causally** by this shared-account pair; B target space below 1R and lower submitted quantity were predicted geometry, while fill order and account state co-change. Stop this H27a child without changing the 0.25-ATR buffer, coin list, risk budget or target on this already exposed year. The next minimal **read-only** diagnostic, if this direction continues, is to bind common-source first/deeper parent quantities and actual native fill/exit chronology for the 1,071 overlapping A/B/clock keys, separating smaller target proceeds from the 66 displaced H26a source opportunities and nine open Positions; do not synthesize alternative fills or Account PnL. A source-grounded, predeclared new mechanism would be needed before another native candidate. All windows here (input 2025-10-07 through 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC, trade from 2025-10-17) have already been viewed; neither this apparent win-rate improvement nor the paired interval is independent validation.

## Diagnostic D95: H27a common-source quantity and native opportunity-path attribution

**Registered before the D95 read:** H27a's failed annual extension creates a specific, falsifiable failure-mechanism question, not a new candidate. The D94 geometric first-tier size ratio was about 0.55 at constant 25-bp bundle risk, but the actual shared-account replay produced 1,071 matched submitted A/B/decision-clock sources, 66 H26a-only sources, two H27a-only sources and a smaller H27a target-profit pool. Test whether the **actual native parent quantities at common submitted sources** were materially smaller under the outside-A stop, and whether native filled/closed trajectories distinguish quantity suppression from different fill/exit states and displaced sources. A prior 14-bar ATR is fixed at the source decision; do not relabel later bars as a new predictor. This is mechanism diagnosis of H27a's failure and does not revive that candidate.

**Frozen inputs, legal comparison and read-only budget:** Use exactly the H27a 37-coin native reports and same-code H26a control frozen in [H27a manifest](results/2026-10-09-h27a-manifest.json), their passing native/clock audits, and the 1,071/66/two source-key partition already reported by [H27a flow](results/2026-10-09-h27a-vs-h26a-flow.json). Verify the report digests before reading. For each submitted two-tier source, link both native ENTRY parents, OTO stop/target siblings, actual fill quantities and times, and native Position final closing order, realized net PnL and open censoring. Pair common sources by coin, rounded A/B and source decision clock **without stop in the key**; pair first/deeper tier by planned entry price, not autogenerated order ID. Report unweighted median and distribution of submitted parent quantity ratios, both-filled-parent ratios, common-source entry fill cross-table (neither/H27a only/H26a only/both), exit-type/PnL sums within truly matched closed/native cohorts, and 66/two unique source outcomes. Keep all 37 coins and all eligibility months. No simulated alternative fills, transformed PnL, price-threshold scan, new Nautilus Strategy or account rerun is allowed. Budget is **one** frozen read-only pass; any repair of mismatched source links must be recorded, not used to select an economic result.

**Falsification and interpretation gate:** If actual common-source first/deeper submitted quantities are not generally lower, the simple wider-stop size mechanism is not supported despite D94 geometry. If target proceeds differ chiefly because target-close count, fill timing, market exits or source admission diverged, report those native path differences instead of assigning the full account gap to size. Common-source native Position PnL cannot be proportionally scaled into a counterfactual because account equity, fees, funding, tier mixing, and year-end open marks depend on the full path. A descriptive decomposition that accounts for the observed gap is still **not** an independent causal effect or a new strategy edge. The annual input window 2025-10-07 to 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC and trade window from 2025-10-17 are fully exposed after many previous trials; this pass carries no validation claim. Stop after reporting native facts, a bounded interpretation and the next smallest source-grounded research question.

**Mechanical identity amendment before D95 output:** The first read aborted before writing a result because one H26a source key (`TIA`, A `0.4287000`, B `0.6259000`, source clock `1768348800000000000`) maps to multiple native Positions. One submitted OTO bundle is therefore not guaranteed to equal exactly one Position over the whole lifecycle. Preserve *every* native Position under each source, count them and classify the source by a multiset of final exit types; sum only actual closed Position PnL and retain any open censor. This repair changes neither the source population nor budget, economic thresholds, or Strategy; the failed assertion produced no selective result artifact.

**D95 frozen native result:** The single [read-only source-path report](results/2026-10-09-d95-h27a-native-path.json), SHA-256 `fdf695c4f792ffdcda0b3a0ab57e15ae096848653c729539c3fb11392c7d0d54`, binds the frozen native summaries/audits and all raw CSV hashes before classifying **1,071** common, **two** H27a-only and **66** H26a-only sources. On common sources, H27a/H26a submitted-parent quantity ratios had median **0.544 first tier** (p10 0.523, p90 0.798) and **0.477 deeper tier** (p10 0.399, p90 0.974). Both versions filled exactly the same **159 first** and **121 deeper** native ENTRY parents, and none was filled in one version only; first-fill timestamps were identical for all 280 paired filled parents. The actual filled-quantity ratios had median **0.547 first** and **0.474 deeper**. The original size-suppression mechanism is therefore supported at the order level, not just geometrically inferred from D94.

At common submitted sources, H27a produced **159 Positions**, H26a **163**: four H26a sources generated two separate native Positions each. **912** sources created no Position in either run. Native final exits on the common cohort were H27a **87 B targets / 51 stops / 12 market-time / nine open**, H26a **71 B targets / 79 stops / five market-time / eight open**. Among **71** sources where both runs closed at B, actual closed realized PnL totaled **+15,050.12 USDT H27a versus +25,854.72 H26a**, a **−10,804.60** difference from those native paths. At **16** sources H27a reached B while H26a stopped (four H26a sources stopped in two Positions), H27a totaled **+4,709.29** versus H26a **−3,600.65**, a **+8,309.95** realized difference. Other both-closed exit-pair differences summed **−993.56**; all both-closed common-source pairs summed **−3,488.21**. These are native cohort bookkeeping, **not** a synthetic account replay or isolated stop effect: an additional common H27a open source was closed as an H26a stop, and year-end unrealized marks are excluded.

The **66 H26a-only submitted sources** yielded 16 Positions, 15 closed (**nine B targets, six stops**) with **+2,546.15 USDT** closed realized PnL and one open; **50** never formed a Position. The two H27a-only sources yielded one stopped Position (**−257.48**) and one unfilled source. Those source differences arise after native shared-account paths diverge, so neither the 66 nor the closed-cohort cash sums can be labeled the independent return of a source filter. The H27a annual account gap remains the exact native **−6,216.68 USDT** relative to H26a; cohort realized sums do not need to reproduce account equity with open marks, funding/account timing and different Position grouping.

**D95 interpretation and next minimum:** The first falsification gate did not fire: common-source order size is systematically lower, while all common entry fill states and first-fill clocks match. A wider stop rescued 16 native source paths but smaller quantities greatly reduced proceeds on the 71 paths that hit the same B target in both versions. This explains the principal *observed* H27a failure mechanism without proving a generalizable causal advantage for H26a, whose source selection and capital path also changed. Do not rescue H27a by raising risk or fitting a farther target to this year. The next smallest source-grounded task is **read-only**: revisit C02/C18/C20 and the exact annotated support-zone definitions to determine whether current wide pivot A is actually the protective boundary or merely the swing anchor; freeze a support-zone construction *from source evidence* and audit its ex-ante geometry and causal availability on all accepted opportunities before proposing any new Strategy rule. Do not call D95 an independent validation, use the 16 rescued trades to choose a zone width, or convert this diagnosis into an unregistered H27b run.

## Source recheck S46: C02 is not an outside-A stop rule

**Retrospective status and scope:** After H27a and D95 results were visible, reread the already archived S01 transcript and original ETH frame, S24 F08 and S27 F32. This is a **post-result source correction**, not a preregistered experiment, an independent source sample or an economic test. The [S46 source digest](results/2026-10-09-s46-stop-source-recheck.json) binds the exact files. No native reports, new prices, orders or PnL were read for S46.

**Evidence and correction:** S01/C02 at 02:10-02:36 discusses an ETH **four-hour rising-line wick** that does not confirm a break without candle-body evidence. At 02:55-03:15 the speaker switches to a **daily descending resistance area** and says a level is a region, not an exact point; the retained daily frame shows that descending resistance. C02 did **not** specify an ETH long stop outside that area. Its older acceptance-case wording and D94's source rationale inadvertently combined line-break, resistance-zone and structural-stop ideas; [C02](SOURCE_CASES.md#c02---wick-and-separate-resistance-zone) now explicitly preserves that correction. The independent S24/C18 BTC F08 says a one-/four-hour stop should at least lie **below 76.4%**, with a horizontal/line support around 61.8% and the wide impulse A further below; D45 already calculated the old 76.4%-buffer stop above A. S27/C20 permits a common stop below a rising line **or** prior low for tier alternatives, but gives no unique line anchors, prior-low selector or cross-asset stop formula. These are different author substyles, not one universal below-A rule.

**Decision and boundary:** H27a's `A - 0.25 prior ATR` remains an honestly registered **researcher proxy** and its poor native account result remains valid for that proxy; S46 narrows the source-fidelity claim, not the score. C02 cannot justify an outside-A stop, C18 provides direct support for a 76.4%-based one-/four-hour stop, and C20 leaves the line/low alternative mechanically underdetermined. D18's previously registered generic support-zone selector also failed an AAVE source case, so it cannot be silently imported as the missing cross-asset boundary. No numeric support-zone construction can be frozen from these three cases without adding researcher assumptions or independently sourced line/zone selection; the next research step must define that source selection and causal clock first, then audit ex-ante geometry before any separate native candidate. Do not backfill S46 as though it preceded H27a, reclassify H27a's 62.25% win as validated, or rerun the exposed year merely because this source interpretation changed.

## Diagnostic D96: does a completed close below the planned 61.8% tier identify post-entry support failure?

**Registered before the D96 native-path read:** C18/S24 treats the 61.8% retracement as one possible BTC support area while allowing a 50% rebound and placing the original one-/four-hour stop below 76.4%. D53 rejected a blanket three-bar no-progress exit, D55 rejected the old four-hour rising-line full exit, and D93 found that a weak close at/below the first fill precedes both second-tier stops and later B winners. None tests the different, source-level event of a *completed four-hour close below the already planned 61.8% deeper tier* while an H26a native Position remains open. The mechanism is that a body below this pre-entry level may distinguish a failed expected support reaction from an ordinary slow rebound. C18 does not prescribe exiting there, and its drawn support area is wider than an exact tick; the proposed exact-level event is explicitly a researcher proxy, with wick-only touches and eventual target winners as counterexamples.

**Frozen identity, legal comparison and causal read:** Bind the exact same-code H26a native shared-account reports at `/tmp/r1-h27a-control-h26a`, their H27a-manifest digests and passed protection/decision-clock audits, and all 37 registered, contiguous five-minute LAST Catalog trees. Retain **every** H26a native Position, including multiple Positions from one source and all year-end opens; reconcile the reported 170 closed/85 positive and nine open before interpretation. Recover both original OTO entry prices from each opening parent bundle and take the **lower planned limit** as the frozen 61.8% level; verify it against the accepted completed-bar source decision and the Instrument tick. From the first actual BUY fill, ignore its incomplete four-hour bar; find the *first later fully completed* four-hour LAST bar closing **strictly below** that lower limit while the native Position remains open after the bar. A low below or a close equal to the limit does not trigger. Positions that stop, target or time-close by that bar are censored by their actual native closing event; year-end opens are right-censored. At the event replay the actual Nautilus Position fills and funding adjustments through that bar, mark the live quantity at its LAST close, subtract one Instrument taker fee for a **descriptive indicative** full exit, and compare with the actual final fee/funding-inclusive native realized PnL only for positions later closed. Report event count, first/second tier state, first-fill R-normalized event close and MFE/MAE through whole post-fill bars, native eventual stop/target/time/open counts, winning and slow-winner counterexamples, and the sum of actual continuation minus indicative exit. Report non-event Positions and censoring. No new fills, synthetic account equity, entry filter, coin/month selection or threshold sweep is permitted.

**Falsification, controls, account metrics and budget:** Refute this exact early-exit lead if fewer than **30** closed native Positions encounter the event or if the fixed-path sum of *indicative exit minus actual continuation* is not **greater than 6,000 USDT**, roughly the H26a-to-20%-annual account-equity gap; record target winners even if the arithmetic gate passes. This is only a capacity screen, not an unbiased treatment effect or executable PnL. Budget **one** read-only pass over the frozen H26a reports/Catalog; repair only auditable identity or event-clock errors, preserving failed attempts. If both screens pass, a separately registered **single** H28a native Strategy child may exit filled exposure at the next actionable native event after that completed close, cancel unfilled tier parents safely, retain original OTO protection until flat and compare with **one exact same-code H26a control** on the same 37 coins, one 100,000-USDT account and identical risk, fee/funding, MARK and LAST data. Its primary metrics would be native final equity/annualized net return (strictly >20% Goal), near-60% closed win, payoff, Sharpe365, daily-close drawdown against H19a's 15.33% ceiling, closed/open/trade count and paired ISO-week uncertainty; no H28a code or run is authorized by D96 alone. If either screen fails, stop this exact below-tier close child; the next extension must identify a different *source-defined structural event*, not tune a bar count, body tolerance or level using this year's winners. The market input from 2025-10-07 through 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC, order eligibility from 2025-10-17, all H26a economics and earlier D53/D93 path reads are already exposed. Any apparent improvement here or in a later same-year native replay is development evidence, never independent validation; delayed/forward evidence and multiplicity accounting remain required.

**D96 readback and decision (after the registered pass):** The frozen read-only result is [`2026-10-09-d96-h26a-tier-support-close.json`](results/2026-10-09-d96-h26a-tier-support-close.json), with [all 179 Position paths](results/2026-10-09-d96-h26a-tier-support-close-positions.json). It was reproduced byte-for-byte after the Node migration from the same H26a native control reports and 37 Catalogs; the existing Node-vs-Engine H26a parity receipt covers the replay implementation. Among 170 closed Positions, 105 encountered the first completed four-hour close below their preplanned 61.8% tier while live. Of those 105, 72 eventually stopped, 30 reached the B target and 3 exited at the time limit; 33 ultimately had positive native realized PnL. The fee-inclusive indicative immediate exit minus actual native continuation summed to **−9,560.36 USDT**, far below the preregistered **>+6,000 USDT** capacity gate. These are descriptive paths, not executable account equity or a causal treatment estimate; year-end opens are censored and actual fills/funding remain Nautilus-owned. The exact below-tier-close exit lead therefore **fails** its registered screen and receives no H28a native candidate. The 30 slow target winners are a direct counterexample to interpreting one close as signal death. A second-level extension must use a different source-defined structural event with its own clock and falsifier, rather than sweep the 61.8% tolerance or bar count on this exposed year. The registration existed on the earlier local R&D branch as `81d7b524c`; its later mainline cherry-pick and this structured transcription do not retroactively make the mainline record pre-result.

## Diagnostic D97: do two strong bear breakout bars after fill identify a failed long?

**Pre-result contract; secondary extension of D96:** D96's one-close price-level event failed its frozen capacity gate and included 30 later B winners. Do not adjust its level, tolerance or observation age. Brooks Trading Course's official [Signs of Strong and Weak Breakouts, Video 15D](https://www.brookstradingcourse.com/wp-content/uploads/2017/02/BTC-Video-15D-Signs-of-Strong-and-Weak-Breakouts.pdf), page 3, describes a strong bear breakout through earlier lows with a substantial bear body, a close near the low and bear follow-through. This is a **different, third-party price-action hypothesis**, not a Ronnie instruction or an exchange order. H23a already froze a bullish strong-bar close in the upper quarter of its range; D97 mirrors that pre-existing fraction for bear bars. The mechanism under test is that two consecutive completed four-hour bearish breakout bars while an H26a native long is still open show persistent opposing order flow, where one below-tier close may merely be a slow winner.

**One fixed observable event:** For each H26a native Position, begin only with complete four-hour LAST bars whose entire interval starts after its first native fill; do not inspect the fill's partial bar. A qualifying bar has `close < open`, `open - close >= mean(abs(open-close))` over its **20 immediately preceding complete four-hour bars**, `close <= low + 0.25*(high-low)`, and `close < previous bar low`. A D97 event occurs at the close of the **second consecutive** qualifying bar, only if the original native Position is still open at that completed-bar decision; the original stop/target/time exit and any intrabar close before that decision censor observation. The 20-bar context and quarter-range rule are researcher encodings of Brooks's qualitative description, fixed now; they are not a claimed author parameter. No 61.8% condition, coin selection, alternate bar count/body fraction, volume test, calendar subgroup or threshold sweep is allowed.

**Falsifier, legal control and stopping rule:** Run **one read-only pass** over the frozen H26a same-code control `/tmp/r1-h27a-control-h26a`, with its 37-Catalog identity, native Position fills, fee/funding adjustments, matched order/Position reports, decision-clock and Node parity receipts checked first. D96's reader may be reused for native reconstruction but D96 event rows must not select D97 cases. Record the event and non-event populations, original stop/target/time closures, year-end open censoring, tier state, first-fill R-normalized event close and MFE/MAE, eventual winners and B winners, and fee-inclusive **indicative** full close at the event versus the actual later native continuation for closed Positions. This is no synthetic account, native fill, counterfactual portfolio return or independent test. Refute this specific exit lead if fewer than **30 closed Positions** have an event or if the sum of indicative exit minus actual continuation is not **greater than +6,000 USDT**; report winner counterexamples even if it passes. Stop this child on either failure, preserving the result and a mechanism-level next question. Only if both gates pass may a separately preregistered **single H28a native child** implement one exit at the next actionable event and compare against the exact same-code H26a control on all 37 coins in one 100,000-USDT Nautilus account, reporting annualized net return, near-60% win, payoff, Sharpe365, daily-close drawdown, trades, fee/funding, paired weeks and multiplicity. H19a's 15.33% annual baseline and strict >20% Goal remain visible. The 2025-10-07 to 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC market window and 2025-10-17 trade start, prior H/D experiments, and D96 paths are already exposed; even a positive same-year result is development evidence awaiting delayed/forward validation.

**D97 one-pass result and failed decision:** Registration commit `8c2b29a81` and reader freeze `74a14d269` preceded the only H26a native-path read. The [aggregate](results/2026-10-09-d97-h26a-bear-followthrough.json) and [179 individual native Position paths](results/2026-10-09-d97-h26a-bear-followthrough-positions.json) bind the unchanged 37 Catalogs, control CSV hashes, audit, decision clock and parity. **42** Positions encountered the fixed two-bar event while still open; **41** eventually closed and one remains right-censored. Of those 41, **21** later stopped, **16** reached B and **four** exited at the time limit; **20** ended with positive fee/funding-inclusive native realized PnL. The event's median age is **27.5 complete post-fill four-hour bars** and median close is about **−0.0945 initial R**. The descriptive, taker-fee-inclusive indicative exit minus actual native continuation sums to **−4,089.74 USDT**. The count gate passes, but the preregistered **>+6,000 USDT** capacity gate fails, so this exact two-strong-bear full-exit child stops without H28a. The 16 later B winners and four profitable time exits are direct counterexamples; the observed strong bear bars often preceded a later recovery rather than persistent invalidation. The fixed-path arithmetic omits feedback on order reservation, future entries and shared-account equity, so it is neither a simulated treatment result nor independent validation. D96 and D97 together narrow two full-exit interpretations; they do **not** dismiss the whole evidence-persistence direction. The next minimal extension should first ask whether an objectively frozen, pre-entry structural support boundary or source-defined reversal *with its own causal clock* adds information beyond these price/bar events; do not mine the 12 later tier fills, event age, R value or this year's winners to tune a cancellation rule.

## Diagnostic D98: pre-fill confirmed local-low support break while H26a remains open

**Pre-result contract:** C20/S27 explicitly allows a prior effective low as one structural boundary for a prospective long, but does not supply a unique cross-asset low selector or instruct a before-stop exit. D96's planned 61.8% line, D97's two strong bear bars and D55's frozen rising-line full exit all failed their own capacity screens; H27a's outside-broad-A stop changed native sizing and missed the Goal. D98 is a different **researcher proxy**, not a Ronnie rule: an already confirmed, still unbroken local swing low strictly between the first native fill and its original native stop may be a more discriminating support failure than either a Fibonacci tier or bearish bar shape. A lower wick alone, an absent eligible pivot, a pivot already closed through, and a later B-target winner are explicit counterexamples.

**Frozen selector and event clock:** Use the exact same-code H26a 37-coin Nautilus control `/tmp/r1-h27a-control-h26a`, its verified native Position fills/OTO stop/funding, and its registered four-hour LAST Catalogs. At each Position's **first actual BUY fill**, identify the last four-hour bar fully completed no later than that fill. Search only the 180 completed bars ending there for order-8 pivot lows: the eight bars on each side must already be complete, the pivot low must equal their minimum, and every later completed close through the fill must be **strictly above** that pivot low. Among pivots with `native original stop < pivot low < first fill price`, freeze the **latest in time**, with earlier index resolving any tie; otherwise record `no_eligible_support` and never add a later pivot. The 180/order-8 scales reuse the H26a broad selector; they are not fitted to D96/D97 outcomes. Start at the first entire four-hour bar after the fill-containing bar. The event is its first completed close **strictly below** the frozen pivot low, provided the original native Position remains open strictly after that close. Actual stop/target/time close by that instant censors the event; a wick or equal close does not trigger. Do not use later fills to select the level or event, and do not change the pivot on later tiers.

**Falsifier, legal control and budget:** One read-only pass over all 179 H26a native Positions and 37 Catalogs, after checking the frozen control's report hashes, native OTO audit, completed-bar clock, input identity and Node parity. Reconcile 170 closed/85 positive and nine year-end open; report eligible/no-support, event/non-event and closure/censoring counts, first-fill R-normalized event close/MFE/MAE, tier state, eventual target/positive counterexamples, real native final fee/funding-inclusive PnL and a **descriptive** LAST-close full exit with one instrument taker fee. Refute this particular full-exit lead if fewer than **30 closed Positions** trigger or if the fixed-path sum of indicative exit minus native continuation is not **strictly above +6,000 USDT**. The arithmetic is a capacity screen, not a counterfactual shared account or execution claim. Budget one reader implementation, one frozen source/control identity check and one 37-coin path read; only fix auditable parser/clock bugs without changing the event. If both gates pass, separately register one H28a native exit/cancel Strategy child and one exact H26a shared-account paired BacktestNode run (plus BTC/ETH integrity pilot), same 100,000 USDT, 37 contracts, LAST/MARK/funding, 25 bp bundle risk and fees; report native annualized net return against strict >20%, win rate near 60%, payoff, Sharpe365, daily-close drawdown against H19a's 15.33%, trades, paired weeks and multiplicity. Failure stops **this** selector, then a distinct source-grounded range/zone or lifecycle question is the minimum secondary extension; do not sweep pivot order, lookback, buffer, n, symbols or calendar on this exposed year. Market data 2025-10-07 through 2026-10-07 08:30 UTC, eligibility from 2025-10-17, prior 37-coin scores, D55/D96/D97 paths and source-video examples are already exposed development data. An apparent gain on them is never independent verification; delayed/forward and multiple-attempt qualification remain necessary.

**One-pass native-path result and decision:** Preregistration `a3566ea70`, binding commit `6aea3effc` and reader freeze `bee698931` preceded the only D98 path read; reader SHA-256 `d08b1859b7ad22911a1588ad40d9ae02f62dbb905d0a3f3c468498eedc0abbf3`. The [aggregate](results/2026-10-09-d98-h26a-local-support.json) SHA-256 `b7f10f18134bb924d059651f586264e0f278624b1cbf9df842d131f17fe2f46f` and [179 Position paths](results/2026-10-09-d98-h26a-local-support-positions.json) SHA-256 `8e1757a347dd9109a608862c2eaf4678c0d2b639942a5e4b2003faf7ef8a6391` bind the existing 37 Catalogs, native reports, order audit, decision clock and same-code Node parity. All **179** original H26a Positions reconcile: **170 closed/85 positive**, nine right-censored open. Only **71** first fills have an eligible pre-fill local-low support; 108 have none. **28** Positions encounter the first complete close below that support while still open, **27** eventually close and one is censored open. Among the 27 closed event Positions, **18** ultimately stop, **eight** reach B and **one** time-exits; **nine** end positive. Median event age is 9.5 complete post-fill four-hour bars, close approximately **−0.420 initial R**. Eight event Positions receive a later original-tier BUY fill. The fee-inclusive descriptive full exit minus native actual continuation sums to **−3,325.43 USDT**. Both preregistered gates fail (27 < 30; −3,325.43 not >+6,000), so no H28a exit/annual replay is admitted from this selector. The eight B-target winners and nine positive closes directly oppose treating this pivot breach as universal signal death. The event cohort, selected by an earlier native fill and survival, cannot estimate a replacement account equity or independent advantage. Next minimum secondary extension is to inspect a source-defined support **area** or pending-tier lifecycle with a new causal selector and explicit slow-winner negatives; no same-year pivot/lookback/buffer sweep.
